The substitution of normalization for receptive field fades as labels switch more often. Where labels run long, neither the receptive-field justification nor the ablation is wrong about its numbers, but both credit the wrong component.

</details>


### [37] [Infrared Universality of Collective Dynamics across Transformer and State-Space Architectures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18592)
*Byung Gyu Chae*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究发现不同神经架构（Transformer和Mamba）尽管微观机制根本不同，却能发展出相似的近边缘慢模态动力学和红外集体组织结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索不同神经架构是否发展出共同的集体动力学。先前研究表明Transformer语言模型存在近平坦、弱红外增强的时间尺度密度（TDOS），本研究旨在测试具有选择性状态空间动力学的Mamba架构是否发展出相关的组织结构。

Method: 在三个层面解析Mamba的弛豫动力学：学习状态空间生成器的内禀谱、输入条件的选择性重标度、以及从雅可比矩阵测量的完整块的集体TDOS。通过累积分析获得谱密度ρ(λ)~λ^β，并对比Transformer全块谱的红外组织。

Result: Mamba全块发展出可复现的慢模态连续体，其红外扇区随序列长度增加而更好地分辨。长序列Mamba指数稳定在β_M≈-0.17，对应记忆动力学K(t)~t^-(1+β)，接近边缘1/t模态。Transformer全块谱表现出密切相关红外组织，代表性指数β_Tr~-0.1。

Conclusion: 显式状态空间记忆与集体红外组织是分离的，不同序列架构可以发展出密切相关的近边缘慢模态动力学。这一发现将红外集体组织扩展到Transformer之外，并为认知场理论描述的动力学结构提供了独立验证。

Abstract: Whether distinct neural architectures develop common collective dynamics remains an open question. Recent analysis of Transformer language models revealed a nearly flat, weakly infrared-enhanced time-scale density of states (TDOS) associated with near-marginal long-memory dynamics. Here we test whether a closely related organization emerges in Mamba, whose selective state-space dynamics provides a fundamentally different microscopic mechanism. Mamba allows relaxation dynamics to be resolved at three levels: the intrinsic spectrum of the learned state-space generator, its input-conditioned selective rescaling, and the collective TDOS of the complete block measured from its Jacobian. These spectra are not identical: selective dynamics and the remaining block transformations substantially reorganize the microscopic relaxation hierarchy. Nevertheless, the full block develops a reproducible slow-mode continuum whose infrared sector becomes progressively better resolved with increasing sequence length. Cumulative analysis yields $ρ(λ)\simλ^β$, with the long-sequence Mamba exponent stabilizing near $β_{\rm M}\simeq-0.17$. The corresponding memory dynamics follows $K(t)\sim t^{-(1+β)}$, close to the marginal $1/t$ regime. Despite fundamentally different microscopic dynamics, Transformer full-block spectra exhibit closely related infrared organization, with representative exponents of order $β_{\rm Tr}\sim-0.1$. These results separate explicit state-space memory from collective infrared organization and show that distinct sequence architectures can develop closely related near-marginal slow-mode dynamics. They extend infrared collective organization beyond Transformers and provide an independent test of the dynamical structure described by Cognitive Field Theory.

</details>


### [38] [Off-Manifold Collapse in Guided Protein Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18597)
*Shuibai Zhang,Xinchi Liu,Fred Zhangzhi Peng,Zhihan Yang,Shutong Wu,Yingzi Ma,Jiawei Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了蛋白质语言模型在推理时引导生成中存在的"离流形坍塌"现象，即生成的激活分布偏离自然分布并退化到低复杂度序列。提出了一种无需训练的后处理过滤方法——马氏距离过滤，通过检测异常激活分布来筛选高质量候选序列，在保持计算成本极低的同时显著提升了生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的蛋白质语言模型推理时引导方法面临困境：温和的引导无法有效改变目标属性，而强烈的引导虽然能改变属性但会导致生成的序列难以折叠。研究发现这种失败具有特定特征——模型自身表示的离流形坍塌，即引导后的激活分布塌缩到与随机氨基酸输入无法区分的区域，序列退化至低复杂度，但优化目标oracle仍可能给出高分，导致无法检测这种坍塌。

Method: 1. 识别并表征"离流形坍塌"现象：通过分析模型激活分布，发现引导后的激活向随机输入区域塌缩

2. 提出马氏距离过滤作为后处理方法：
   - 在自然蛋白质激活上构建廉价密度先验
   - 计算候选序列激活与自然分布的马氏距离
   - 仅保留在自然分布下典型的候选序列

3. 该方法无需训练、不修改生成器、可直接应用于现有引导方法的输出

Result: 实验结果表明：
1. 离流形坍塌具有可检测的特征，引导后的激活分布与随机输入统计上无法区分
2. 马氏距离过滤在相同引导设置下同时提升了属性分数和结构合理性
3. 方法计算成本极低（可忽略），且可跨不同引导方法迁移
4. 对于溶解性任务，oracle可能主动奖励坍塌，但结构和组成分析能暴露失败

Conclusion: 本研究揭示了蛋白质序列设计中推理引导方法的根本性失效机制，即离流形坍塌现象。所提出的马氏距离过滤方法为解决该问题提供了一个简单、有效、通用的后处理方案，通过在输出端检测而非修改生成器的方式，成功平衡了属性优化与序列自然性之间的矛盾，为蛋白质设计提供了更可靠的推理时引导策略。

Abstract: Protein language models are widely used priors for protein sequence design, and a growing body of work controls them at inference time as an alternative to fine-tuning. Such guidance faces a dilemma: mild enough to preserve natural activation statistics, it barely moves the property; strong enough to move it, the generations become progressively harder to fold. We show the failure has a specific and cheaply detectable signature, an off-manifold collapse of the model's own representations. Guided activations fall toward a region statistically indistinguishable from random amino-acid input, and the sequences degenerate to low complexity, yet the property oracle being optimized can still score these generations as a success. The optimized oracle can therefore fail to witness the collapse and, for solubility, can actively reward it, whereas structure and composition expose the failure. Because the failure is already visible in a finished candidate, we detect it at the output rather than modify the generator. We introduce a cheap density prior over natural protein activations and keep only the candidates that remain typical under it, a training-free post-hoc step we call Mahalanobis filtering. At matched guidance settings it improves both the property score and the structural plausibility of the sequences it keeps at negligible cost, without touching the generator, and transfers across different guidance methods. We release the activation statistic at https://huggingface.co/Shuibai12138/off-manifold-collapse-plm

</details>


### [39] [Denoising-Aware Inversion: Revealing Privacy Risks in Noise-Protected Text Embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18610)
*Yubo Wang,Shujie Cui,James Bailey,Hongzhi Yin,Wenyu Liang,Min Tang,Shiyue Qin,Weiqing Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文挑战了高斯噪声可有效保护文本嵌入隐私的假设，提出了DAEI方法成功破解噪声保护的嵌入，表明简单噪声防御并不安全。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究表明文本嵌入可能泄露隐私信息，常用防御方法是通过添加高斯噪声来扰动嵌入。然而，目前尚不清楚这种噪声保护的嵌入在面对能够显式考虑扰动过程的自适应攻击者时是否足够安全，本文旨在研究这一噪声保护设置下的嵌入逆向攻击问题。

Method: 首先分析现有生成式逆向方法在噪声保护设置下失败的原因，发现存在"双重噪声陷阱"阻碍高质量重建。然后提出DAEI（去噪感知嵌入逆向）流水线，结合残差去噪自编码器与生成式文本逆向，其中去噪器使用Stein无偏风险估计以无监督方式进行训练，仅从噪声观测中实现去噪。

Result: 大量实验表明，DAEI相比现有生成式逆向基线在BLEU指标上实现了约154%的相对提升，同时在token级F1和ROUGE-L指标上提升了32-60%。这表明DAEI能够有效地从噪声保护的嵌入中重建原始文本。

Conclusion: DAEI的出色逆向性能挑战了普遍假设，即简单的高斯扰动足以防止嵌入表示中的敏感信息泄露。研究结果表明仅靠高斯噪声防御并不能提供足够的隐私保护，需要更强大的防御机制来确保嵌入的安全性。

Abstract: Dense text embeddings are widely used in data mining, retrieval, and downstream machine learning systems due to their compact and semantically rich representations, but recent embedding inversion attacks have shown that they can expose substantial information about the original text, leading to serious privacy leakage risks. A common defense is to release perturbed embeddings by adding Gaussian noise, which is simple yet effective against standard inversion attacks and does not significantly degrade embedding utility for downstream tasks. However, it remains unclear whether such noise-protected embeddings are sufficiently safe against adaptive attackers that explicitly account for the perturbation process. In this paper, we study text embedding inversion in a noise-protected setting, where the attacker can observe only noisy embeddings and has no access to clean embedding targets. We first analyze why existing generative inversion methods fail under this setting and identify a "Double Noise Trap", which fundamentally prevents standard generative inversion models from achieving high-quality reconstruction. To address this challenge, we propose DAEI, a denoising-aware embedding inversion pipeline that combines a residual denoising autoencoder with generative text inversion where the denoiser is trained in an unsupervised manner using Stein's unbiased risk estimate to enable denoising from noisy observations alone. Extensive experiments show that DAEI achieves approximately 154\% relative improvement in BLEU over the existing generative inversion baseline, while also improving token-level F1 and ROUGE-L by 32--60\%. The promising inversion performance of DAEI challenges the prevailing assumption that simple Gaussian perturbation is sufficient to prevent sensitive information leakage from embedding representations.

</details>


### [40] [Scalable Geospatial Machine Learning for Power-Line Asset Risk: Integrating Remote Sensing for Lightning and Vegetation Risk Modelling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18611)
*Artur Sokolovsky,Bhavik Merai,Moe Jafari,Muen Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一个模块化、可扩展且可解释的电力资产故障概率(PoF)建模框架，用于资产级、空间显式的风险评估，支持电力网络针对天气敏感型故障的有效干预规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电力网络日益暴露于天气敏感型故障机制中，需要资产级、空间显式的风险建模来支持有效的干预规划。在工业环境中，风险模型必须在适应数据可用性、资产管理优先级和气候驱动灾害条件变化的同时，保持运营上的可维护性。

Method: 构建了资产级模块化架构的故障概率建模框架，采用协调的地理空间机器学习流水线，整合多源预测因子，包括地形数据(SRTM)、植被状况(MODIS NDVI)、雷电气候学(LIS VHRMC)、OpenStreetMap衍生的邻近特征以及公用事业运营记录。

Result: 该框架在植被相关和雷电相关故障模式上得到了验证，所构建的架构计算效率高、操作可扩展，适用于公用事业规模部署，能够提供可操作的资产级风险分层。

Conclusion: 该框架为巡检优先级排序、植被管理、资产加固和韧性规划提供了可操作的资产级风险分层支持，有助于更早的干预和更具气候韧性的网络运营，支持公用事业公司在变化的环境条件下维持风险模型的运营可维护性。

Abstract: Electric power networks are increasingly exposed to weather-sensitive failure mechanisms that require asset-level, spatially explicit risk modelling for effective intervention planning. This study contributes a modular, robust, and explainable probability-of-failure (PoF) modelling framework for utility asset management. The central contribution is an asset-level architecture that can be scaled to new environmental data sources and additional PoF types without reworking the underlying pipeline. This is particularly relevant for industry settings, where risk models must remain operationally maintainable while adapting to changing data availability, asset-management priorities, and climate-driven hazard conditions. We demonstrate the framework for vegetation-related and lightning-related failure modes using a harmonised geospatial machine-learning pipeline. The implementation integrates multi-source predictors, including topography (SRTM), vegetation condition (MODIS Normalised Difference Vegetation Index - NDVI), lightning climatology (LIS VHRMC), OpenStreetMap-derived proximity features, and utility operational records. The resulting architecture is computationally efficient, operationally extensible, and suitable for utility-scale deployment. It provides actionable asset-level risk stratification for inspection prioritisation, vegetation management, asset hardening, and resilience planning, supporting earlier intervention and more climate-resilient network operations.

</details>


### [41] [Coordination on a Budget: Federated Active Learning with Few Labels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18634)
*Liam Mohr,Daphna Weinshall*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在联邦主动学习的低预算场景下，同构(IID)数据反而比异构数据更具挑战性。本文提出了一种基于联邦表示学习的框架，通过在共享嵌入空间中对齐客户端数据，实现了全局协调的主动选择，在隐私约束下取得了超越现有方法的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦主动学习面临数据隐私和标签稀缺的双重挑战，而在缺乏全局数据视图的情况下进行协调查询选择更加困难。现有研究主要关注异构数据的挑战，但在低预算场景下的查询选择机制尚不明确。

Method: 提出了一种新的FAL框架，利用联邦表示学习将客户端数据对齐到共享嵌入空间中。这使得服务器能够对经过可选混淆的客户端嵌入执行全局协调的主动选择，而标注仍在每个客户端本地进行。该方法特别针对低预算场景设计。

Result: 在理论上和实证上发现了"异构性逆转"现象：在低预算设置下，同构(IID)数据需要更强的协调来避免冗余查询，而异构数据自然促进多样性；这一趋势在高预算时反转。所提出的框架在更具挑战性的低预算场景下，即使现有方法被给予更大的标注预算，仍能超越它们的性能。

Conclusion: 与标准联邦学习中异构性是主要挑战的观点相反，本文证明了在FAL的查询选择中IID设置更具挑战性。通过联邦表示学习实现全局协调，可以在保护隐私的前提下显著提升低预算场景下的主动学习性能。

Abstract: Federated Active Learning (FAL) addresses the dual challenges of data privacy and label scarcity, where the absence of a global data view introduces additional hurdles for coordinated query selection. We study cross-silo FAL in the low-budget regime, where annotation decisions are most critical. We characterize, both theoretically and empirically, a heterogeneity reversal: in low-budget settings, homogeneous (IID) data requires stronger coordination to avoid redundant queries, whereas heterogeneous data naturally promotes diversity; this trend reverses at higher budgets. Thus, in contrast to the standard federated learning (FL) narrative where heterogeneity is a primary challenge, we show that IID settings are more challenging for query selection in FAL.
  Motivated by these findings, we propose a new FAL framework that utilizes federated representation learning to align client data in a shared embedding space. This enables the server to perform globally coordinated active selection over optionally obfuscated client embeddings, while annotation remains local to each client. Although our framework operates in the more challenging low-budget regime, it achieves performance that surpasses existing FAL methods even when they are given substantially larger annotation budgets, demonstrating the value of centralized coordination under privacy constraints.

</details>


### [42] [ProxyGuard: Direct Reliability Inference for Randomized Data Release Mechanisms with Shared Targets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18643)
*Dipesh Tharu Mahato,Pramod Dhungana*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ProxyGuard方法，通过预设风险界限和封闭目标集，解决了在众多发布版本中选择代理数据集时的两类错误问题，并提供了无需独立目标批次或发布级p值依赖假设的有限样本机制可靠性保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究人员经常从多个发布版本、变换或种子中选择代理数据集。搜索可能使无效版本看起来足够，而一个足够的版本并不能证明其生成器是可靠的。现有方法无法同时控制这两类错误，且缺乏对机制可靠性的有效保证。

Method: 提出ProxyGuard方法，包含两种模式：(1)命名发布模式：校正多重性并认证特定发布版本；(2)直接共享目标模式：评估独立机制抽取在共同目标上的表现，下界化有利得分率，减去无效发布贡献的有利得分界限。利用条件独立性质，无需独立目标批次或发布级p值依赖假设。推导出仅均值惩罚是精确的，并导出具有可加性目标集中的平滑得分证书。

Result: 在注册的三要求研究中，直接模式在可靠性0.95水平下将功效从5.6%提升至64.2%；命名模式在高信号证据下表现更强。方法成功应用于全流程Rice-TVAE（每次抽取重新训练）和非表格文本机制的前瞻性审计。

Conclusion: ProxyGuard提供了首个能在有限样本下同时控制选择有效性和机制可靠性错误的方法，无需强分布假设，为数据代理选择和机制审计提供了统计严格的理论保证和实践工具。

Abstract: Researchers often choose a proxy dataset from many releases, transformations, or seeds. Search can make an invalid release appear adequate, while one adequate release does not establish that its generator is reliable. ProxyGuard controls both errors using prespecified bounded risks and a sealed target set. Named-release mode corrects for multiplicity and certifies specific releases. Direct shared-target mode evaluates independent mechanism draws on a common target, lower-bounds their favorable-score rate, and subtracts a bound on favorable scores contributed by invalid releases. Conditional on the target, release scores are independent, yielding a finite-sample mechanism-reliability guarantee without independent target batches or assumptions on release-level $p$-value dependence. We show that the mean-only penalty is sharp and derive a smooth-score certificate with additive target concentration. In a registered three-requirement study, direct mode raises power from 5.6\% to 64.2\% at reliability 0.95, while named mode remains stronger under high-signal evidence. Prospective audits span full-pipeline Rice--TVAE, which retrains on every draw, and a non-tabular text mechanism.

</details>


### [43] [FlashAttention for Scalable Vector Architectures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18656)
*Sonia Rani Gupta,Nikela Papadopoulou,Miquel Pericàs*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了FlashAttention-V，一种面向可扩展向量架构的分块FlashAttention实现，在CPU上实现了显著的性能提升（预填充阶段22-42倍，解码阶段8-11倍），并揭示了当前量化格式在长向量执行中的根本性瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CPU上Transformer模型的推理日益重要，特别是针对小语言模型（SLM），向量架构正成为有前景的执行平台。注意力模块因高内存带宽需求成为主要瓶颈，而FlashAttention通过融合操作改善了数据局部性。现有方案在向量架构上的可扩展性不足，需要针对不同向量长度进行优化。

Method: 提出FlashAttention-V：采用分块FlashAttention设计，通过注意力头间的并行性、头间打包技术实现超出头维度的向量长度高效利用，改进向量寄存器利用率和内存访问局部性。集成到ggml/llama.cpp中，在TinyLlama、Llama 3.2、Qwen2.5和Pythia-410M上进行评估，使用gem5模拟器和Banana Pi BPI-F3硬件平台验证。

Result: 在512位向量长度下，预填充阶段相比标量FlashAttention实现22-42倍加速，扩展至64通道/4096位向量长度时额外获得2-2.5倍提升。解码阶段在512位向量长度下实现8-11倍加速。确认循环重排序和注意力头间循环展开是有效的优化原则。发现Q8_0量化线性层在长向量执行中存在结构性瓶颈，限制算术分摊。

Conclusion: FlashAttention-V有效解决了向量架构上注意力模块的性能瓶颈，实现了显著加速。同时揭示了当前量化格式在长向量执行中的根本性挑战，为未来优化指明了方向。性能收益随模型规模增大而提升，在短上下文和解码阶段最为显著。

Abstract: Inference with transformer models on CPUs is increasingly important, especially for Small Language Models (SLMs), where vector architectures are emerging as a promising execution substrate. The attention module is a major bottleneck due to high memory bandwidth requirements; FlashAttention mitigates this by fusing operations to improve data locality and reduce intermediate memory traffic. In this paper, we present FlashAttention-V, a blocked FlashAttention for scalable vector architectures that adapts efficiently from short to very long vectors by exploiting parallelism across attention heads, inter-head packing to enable efficient utilization of vector lengths beyond the head dimension, and improving vector register utilization and memory access locality. We integrate FlashAttention-V into ggml within llama.cpp and evaluate it on TinyLlama, Llama 3.2, Qwen2.5, and Pythia-410M using gem5 and a Banana Pi BPI-F3. On the Banana Pi BPI-F3, we confirm that loop reordering and loop unrolling across attention heads are effective optimization principles, scaling performance gains with larger models and most pronounced with short contexts and during decoding. Simulation-based analysis shows that FlashAttention-V achieves 22x-42x speedup over scalar FlashAttention at 512-bit VL in prefill, with an additional 2x-2.5x gain scaling to 64 lanes and 4096-bit VL. During decode, FlashAttention-V achieves 8x-11x speedup using 512-bit vector lengths over scalar FlashAttention, with performance showing diminishing sensitivity to vector width and lane count due to single-token, memory-bound execution. We further identify structural bottlenecks in Q8_0 quantized linear layers that limit arithmetic amortization under long-vector execution, consistent across RVV and Arm SVE, indicating that current quantization formats pose a fundamental challenge to long-vector scalability.

</details>


### [44] [Computational Measurement of Team-Process Phase Dynamics in Collaborative Virtual Reality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18660)
*Qing Huang,Jianing Zhang,Pooja Pol*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个计算框架，用于从协作VR游戏中的时间戳对话数据中检测和解释动态团队过程阶段，通过结合表示学习、变化点检测和LLM辅助解释，实现了对团队协作时序变化的透明可追溯分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有协作VR环境中的团队沟通分析通常采用整体摘要或固定时间窗口的方法，这种方法会掩盖团队沟通和协调随时间的动态变化特征，无法捕捉团队过程的真实演变规律。

Method: 框架采用三阶段方法：(1) 使用late chunking生成上下文感知的对话表示，聚合成时间块；(2) 应用惩罚高斯核变化点检测识别语义转换点；(3) 结合TF-IDF、NMF和代表性对话片段提供结构化证据，使用本地部署的LLM通过上下文学习生成初步解释，由人工审查后与独立记录的交互日志对齐分析任务行为模式。

Result: 实验评估表明，该框架能够识别出连贯且可解释的阶段结构，同时保持与原始对话证据的可追溯性；对话派生的阶段与交互行为之间的对应关系进一步支持了其在协作活动分析中的相关性。

Conclusion: 该框架为研究协作任务场景中基于时间戳对话的团队工作时序变化提供了一种透明且可迁移的方法，具有广泛的应用前景。

Abstract: Collaborative virtual reality (VR) environments make team communication observable as it unfolds, but conventional transcript analyses often summarize entire trials or divide them into fixed temporal windows. Such approaches can obscure changes in team communication and coordination over time. This article presents a computational framework for detecting and interpreting dynamic team-process phases from timestamped dialogue in a collaborative VR game. The framework uses late chunking to generate context-aware transcript representations, aggregates them into temporal chunks, and applies penalized Gaussian-kernel change-point detection to identify semantic transitions in team communication. After boundary detection, term frequency--inverse document frequency (TF-IDF), non-negative matrix factorization (NMF), and representative transcript segments provide structured evidence for phase interpretation. A locally deployed large language model (LLM) uses in-context learning to generate initial interpretations that are subsequently reviewed by humans. Independently recorded interaction logs are then aligned with the detected phases to examine corresponding task-action patterns. The evaluation compares representations, pooling strategies, segmentation methods, parameter settings, reviewed phase interpretations, and phase-aligned interaction profiles. The results show that the framework identifies coherent and interpretable phase structures while preserving traceability to the underlying transcript evidence. The correspondence between transcript-derived phases and interaction behavior further supports their relevance for analyzing collaborative activity. The framework therefore offers a transparent and transferable approach for studying temporal changes in teamwork from timestamped transcripts across collaborative task settings.

</details>


### [45] [Reinforced Planning with Latent World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18669)
*Armin Sommer,Jannik Schilling*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出强化规划（Reinforced Planning）方法RP1，首次实现了对多步规划的改进过程的完全学习。RP1通过强化学习训练神经规划器，学习评估想象结果和改进多步规划，在多个任务上显著优于手工设计的搜索算法，同时大幅降低了计算成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前世界模型能够预测动作序列的结果，但候选规划的改进仍未被完全学习。现有的规划器要么是手工设计，要么从手工设计的优化器中蒸馏得到，或者仅用于通知摊销策略而非改进规划本身。缺乏能够完全学习如何改进多步规划的方法。

Method: 提出强化规划方法RP1，通过强化学习将良好的搜索规则嵌入到神经规划器中。RP1包含两个核心组件：1）通过评论家（critic）学习评估想象结果；2）通过优化器学习改进多步规划，该优化器完全从想象的世界模型推演中进行离线训练。RP1可以独立训练并附加到任何预训练的潜在世界模型上。

Result: 在视觉导航、机械臂到达和机器人操作任务上，使用两个世界模型骨干网络进行测试。RP1显著优于手工设计的搜索算法，在多个场景中达到近乎完美的成功率，同时使用的世界模型推演次数减少1000倍，在并发规划器推理下比最强替代方法快达67倍。

Conclusion: RP1是首个完全学习如何改进多步规划的方法，证明了通过强化学习训练神经规划器的有效性。该方法具有通用性，可与任意预训练潜在世界模型结合，在性能和效率上都取得了显著提升，为学习型规划器的发展开辟了新方向。

Abstract: Humans solve complex problems by constructing plans and mentally simulating their outcomes with an internal model of the world. Machine learning has produced world models that similarly predict the outcomes of action sequences, but the improvement of candidate plans still isn't fully learned. Current planners are either hand-designed, distilled from a hand-designed optimizer, or learned only to inform an amortized policy rather than to revise the plan itself. We introduce the Reinforced Planning, a method based on the idea that search can be learned by reinforcing good search rules into a neural planner. Our implementation RP1 learns both how to evaluate imagined outcomes through a critic, as well as how to improve multi-step plans through an optimizer trained fully offline from imagined world-model roll-outs. To our knowledge, RP1 is the first method to fully learn how to improve multi-step plans. Furthermore, it can be trained independently of and attached to any pretrained latent world model. Across visual navigation, arm reaching, and robotic manipulation on two world-model backbones, RP1 substantially outperforms hand-designed search algorithms, reaching near-perfect success in several settings while using $1,000 \times$ less world-model rollouts and being up to $67 \times$ faster than the strongest alternative under concurrent planner inference.

</details>


### [46] [An Empirical Benchmark of Deep Time-Series Models for Smart Meter Energy Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18675)
*Behnaz Kavoosighafi,Maria Eidenskog,Wiktoria Glad,Katerina Vrotsou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究对比评估了九种深度学习模型在智能电表能耗预测任务上的表现，发现深度学习模型优于传统基线，轻量级架构在低成本下表现接近复杂模型，而历史输入长度和预测范围是影响性能的关键因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准确的能耗预测对电力系统的高效运行、成本管理和系统维护至关重要。虽然智能电表提供了大量高分辨率数据，但数据驱动方法在真实智能电表数据上的对比性能研究仍然不足，缺乏对不同深度学习架构在实际应用中表现的系统性评估。

Method: 研究实现了九种现代深度学习时间序列预测模型的基准测试，涵盖线性模型、MLP架构、卷积网络和Transformer架构。在两个公开智能电表数据集上进行评估，重点分析三个关键因素：历史输入长度、预测范围和模型架构选择，并研究预测精度与计算复杂度之间的权衡。

Result: 研究发现：延长历史输入可提高预测精度，但存在饱和点，超过后收益有限；预测范围增加会导致精度下降。深度学习模型持续优于传统基线方法，而轻量级架构以显著更低的计算成本实现与复杂模型相近的性能。架构差异仅在较长预测范围和更异质数据集上才显现意义。大多数人口细分群体对模型选择不敏感。

Conclusion: 研究表明深度学习在能耗预测中具有明显优势，但并非越复杂的模型越好。轻量级架构在实际应用中更具性价比，模型架构选择应基于具体的应用场景和数据特征。该研究为智能电表能耗预测的模型选择提供了实用的指导建议。

Abstract: Accurate forecasting of energy consumption is important for the efficient operation of power systems, with direct implications for operational costs, energy management, and system maintenance. Due to the availability of extensive high-resolution consumption data from smart meters, data-driven methods have been used for short-term and long-term forecasting. However, their comparative performance on real-world smart meter data is still not well studied. In this paper, we present an empirical benchmark of nine modern deep learning models for time-series forecasting, including linear, MLP-based, convolutional, and Transformer architectures. We evaluate these models on two publicly available smart meter datasets. Our analysis focuses on three factors that strongly affect forecasting performance: the length of historical input, the prediction horizon, and the choice of model architecture. We show that extending the historical context improves accuracy, but only up to a saturation point, after which additional input provides limited benefit. In contrast, accuracy decreases as the prediction horizon increases. We also investigate the trade-off between prediction accuracy and computational complexity, and assess the statistical significance and practical magnitude of performance differences across models. Our results show that deep learning models consistently outperform classical baselines, while lightweight architectures achieve relatively similar performance at significantly lower computational cost. Additionally, architectural differences only become meaningful at longer forecasting horizons and on more heterogeneous datasets. Finally, a subgroup analysis across geodemographic and household categories shows that model choice has limited impact for most population segments.

</details>


### [47] [Transforming Heart Disease Prediction with Advanced Machine Learning Techniques](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18687)
*Sami Ullah,Muhammad Mohsin Khan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究通过比较多种机器学习分类器在UCI和Kaggle数据集上的表现，发现SVM在UCI数据集上表现最佳，Simple Cart在Kaggle数据集上效果最好，验证了机器学习模型可有效辅助心脏病早期诊断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 心脏病是全球致死主要原因，迫切需要早期准确检测以改善患者预后。机器学习技术在医疗诊断领域展现出巨大潜力，但不同分类器的性能差异显著，需要系统性比较研究来确定最优方法。

Method: 使用来自UCI和Kaggle的两个心脏病数据集（各含14个属性），应用9种分类器：J48、朴素贝叶斯、逻辑回归、Simple Cart、Bagging、Decision Stump、AdaBoost、人工神经网络和支持向量机。采用MAE、RAE、准确率、精确率、召回率和F-measure等指标进行全面性能评估与比较。

Result: SVM分类器在UCI数据集上获得最佳性能，而Simple Cart分类器在Kaggle数据集上表现最优，两者均实现了最高的准确率和最低的误差率。

Conclusion: 经过适当调优和验证的机器学习模型能够显著辅助心脏病早期诊断，为临床决策提供关键支持。未来研究可探索混合方法和更新数据集以进一步提升预测准确性。

Abstract: Heart disease remains the leading cause of mortality globally, necessitating early and accurate detection to improve patient outcomes. This research focuses on the predictive analysis of heart disease using machine learning (ML) techniques, comparing the performance of multiple classifiers to identify the most accurate and least error-prone method. Two datasets from UCI and Kaggle repositories were utilized, each containing 14 attributes related to heart health indicators. Techniques including J48, Naive Bayes, Logistic Regression, Simple Cart, Bagging, Decision Stump, AdaBoost, Artificial Neural Networks, and Support Vector Machine (SVM) were applied. Evaluation metrics such as Mean Absolute Error (MAE), Relative Absolute Error (RAE), accuracy, precision, recall, and F-measure were used for performance comparison. Results revealed that SVM achieved the highest performance on the UCI dataset, while Simple Cart performed best on the Kaggle dataset, offering the highest accuracy and lowest error rates. The research work concludes that ML models, when properly tuned and validated, can significantly assist in the early diagnosis of heart disease, offering critical support for clinical decision-making. Future work may involve hybrid approaches and the use of more recent datasets to further improve prediction accuracy.

</details>


### [48] [Europe's Climate Ambition Under Scrutiny: Evidence from Deep Learning Emission Projections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18690)
*Jacopo Ghirri,Carlos Rodriguez-Pardo,Lara Aleluia Reis,Massimo Tavoni*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 利用深度学习预测欧盟27国2030年CO₂排放将超标35%，仅少数国家达标；电力部门进展良好，交通部门因结构性惯性进展缓慢。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 欧盟承诺到2030年温室气体排放量相比1990年减少55%，但目前趋势是否与这一目标相一致仍存在不确定性，需要数据驱动的预测来评估当前进展。

Method: 应用深度学习方法，使用截至2023年的高分辨率社会经济和部门数据，预测欧盟27国在当前趋势下的部门CO₂排放轨迹，基于观察到的部门动量进行外推，不假设政策环境有额外变化。

Result: 预测EU27排放量将超过2030年目标35%（短缺620 Mt CO₂），仅少数国家轨迹与承诺一致。电力部门因可再生能源转型实现目标一致的减排，而交通部门进展甚微，到2030年占总排放量三分之一以上，且结构性惯性遍布各成员国。

Conclusion: 欧洲需要采取大量额外干预措施来缩小雄心-实施差距，并呼吁在欧洲建立最新的能源信息体系。

Abstract: The European Union has committed to reducing greenhouse gas emissions 55% below 1990 levels by 2030, but whether current trends are compatible with this ambition remains uncertain. We apply deep learning to high-resolution socioeconomic and sectoral data across EU27 member states till 2023 to project sectoral CO$_2$ trajectories under current trends, extrapolating observed sectoral momentum without assuming changes in the pace or effectiveness of the policy environment beyond what is already reflected in historical data. We project that EU27 emissions will exceed the 2030 target by 35% (620 Mt CO$_2$ shortfall), with only a small minority of countries on trajectories consistent with the bloc's commitments. While the Power sector achieves target-consistent reductions driven by the renewable transition, Mobility shows minimal progress and accounts for over a third of total emissions by 2030, reflecting a structural inertia across member states rather than geographically concentrated lag. Our findings indicate that substantial additional intervention is required to close Europe's ambition-implementation gap, and call for establishing up-to-date energy information in Europe.

</details>


### [49] [Multi-Class Electrical and Mechanical Fault Classification Using Random Convolutional Kernels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18716)
*Mouhamadou Mansour Lo,Mouad Talbaoui,Gildas Morvan,Mathieu Rossi,Fabrice Morganti,David Mercier*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文评估了SelF-Rocket在旋转机械机械和电气故障多类诊断中的性能，并首次提出了多变量扩展版本，该方法在准确性与延迟间取得了最佳平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旋转机械故障诊断对工业过程可靠性至关重要，基于随机卷积核的时间序列分类方法（如ROCKET）在预测性能与计算效率间提供了良好的权衡，需要评估其在实际故障诊断中的表现并进行扩展。

Method: 评估SelF-Rocket方法在机械和电气故障多类诊断中的性能，并提出该方法的多元扩展版本；在MaFaulDa（机械故障）和ITSC-UDG（定子匝间短路）两个公开基准数据集上，分别在单变量和多变量设置下与领先的ROCKET方法进行比较。

Result: 在评估的方法中，SelF-Rocket实现了最佳的总体准确性-延迟权衡；在MaFaulDa数据集上获得了最高的分类性能，在更具挑战性的ITSC-UDG数据集上保持高度竞争力。

Conclusion: SelF-Rocket及其多变量扩展版本在旋转机械故障诊断中表现出色，能够有效平衡分类准确性和计算延迟，适用于实际工业应用场景。

Abstract: Diagnosing faults in rotating machinery is essential for ensuring the reliability of industrial processes. Random convolutional kernel-based Time Series Classification (TSC) methods, such as ROCKET and its variants, provide an attractive trade-off between predictive performance and computational efficiency. In this work, we evaluate SelF-Rocket for the multi-class diagnosis of both mechanical and electrical faults and introduce, as a new contribution, a multivariate extension of the original method. The proposed approach is compared with leading ROCKET-based methods on two public benchmark datasets, MaFaulDa (mechanical faults) and ITSC-UDG (stator inter-turn short circuits), under both univariate and multivariate settings. Experimental results show that SelF-Rocket achieves the best overall accuracy-latency trade-off among the evaluated methods, obtaining the highest classification performance on MaFaulDa while remaining highly competitive on the more challenging ITSC-UDG dataset.

</details>


### [50] [Visual-Aware Representation of Web Pages for Machine Learning Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18727)
*Radek Burget,Radek Hranický*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个基于FitLayout的视觉感知网页表示与机器学习平台，通过渲染网页并捕获其视觉和结构特性，解决了HTML网页机器学习中的表示难题，并使用图神经网络演示了其在识别关键内容元素方面的应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将机器学习应用于网页内容具有挑战性，因为需要解释HTML及其关联资源，并进行渲染以获得有意义的视觉和布局感知表示。因此，基于网页内容的机器学习研究相对较少。

Method: 基于开源渲染工具FitLayout构建了一个平台，提供服务器端网页渲染功能，将视觉和结构属性显式地捕获为基于RDF的表示，并将渲染后的文档持久化存储。平台通过REST API控制处理流程，使用SPARQL查询检索适合作为机器学习算法输入的结构化数据，并提供Python客户端库与标准机器学习工作流集成。

Result: 该平台支持从网页收集、渲染到内容元素预处理和标注再到下游学习任务的完整数据集准备流程，能够实现渲染网页的显式建模（包括细粒度的布局细节），支持数据集共享和实验结果的可复现性。通过演示展示了如何将渲染网页转换为图表示并训练图神经网络用于识别关键内容元素。

Conclusion: 该平台成功实现了网页内容的视觉感知表示，支持完整的机器学习工作流，证明了方法的适用性和结果的可复现性，为网页内容的机器学习研究提供了新的可能性。

Abstract: Applying machine learning to web pages is challenging due to the need to interpret HTML together with associated resources and perform rendering to obtain a meaningful visual and layout-aware representation. As a result, machine learning over web content remains comparatively underexplored. In this paper, we present a platform for visual-aware representation and machine learning over web pages based on the open-source rendering tool FitLayout. The platform provides a server capable of rendering web pages, explicitly capturing their visual and structural properties in an RDF-based representation, and persisting the rendered documents in an integrated storage. The processing pipeline is controlled via a REST API, while SPARQL queries are used to retrieve structured data suitable as input for machine learning algorithms. By explicitly modeling rendered web pages, including fine-grained layout details, the platform enables dataset sharing and supports the reproducibility of experimental results. The architecture supports the complete dataset preparation workflow, from web page collection and rendering through preprocessing and annotation of content elements to downstream learning tasks. We further provide a Python client library that integrates the platform with standard machine learning workflows. As a demonstration, we show how rendered web pages can be transformed into graph-based representations and used to train graph neural networks for recognizing key content elements, illustrating both the applicability of the approach and the reproducibility of the results.

</details>


### [51] [Decision-Metric Alignment in Latent World Models: Diagnostics and Action-Conditioned Objectives for MPC Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18746)
*Jiawei Wang,Ke Rui,Yushen Zuo,Yichun Feng,Minglei Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出DA-LeWM方法，通过添加逆动力学和示范条件目标动作头，改善JEPA式潜在世界模型在MPC中的决策度量对齐性，显著加速收敛并提高在线成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: JEPA式潜在世界模型使用欧几里得距离作为MPC代价函数，但潜在空间中的距离排序不能保证反映真实任务进度，存在决策度量对齐问题。

Method: 引入Plan-Real Spearman和CEM-stage Spearman指标测量潜在-真实排序一致性；识别编码器畸变、终端展开误差和候选边距为控制量；提出DA-LeWM，在LeWM基础上增加逆动力学和示范条件目标动作头。

Result: DA-LeWM在所有实验中加速收敛，相比LeWM获得更高的在线成功率，探测分数保持相似水平。

Conclusion: 动作条件目标能改善基于欧几里得代价、CEM搜索的潜在MPC所使用的几何结构，验证了改善决策度量对齐的有效性。

Abstract: JEPA-style latent world models can use Euclidean distance to a goal latent as the cost for model-predictive control (MPC). Strong decoding of task variables, however, does not guarantee that this particular cost ranks candidate action sequences by real task progress. We call the latter property \emph{decision-metric alignment}. We introduce Plan-Real Spearman, which measures latent--real rank agreement on random plans, and CEM-stage Spearman, which measures the same agreement as cross-entropy-method (CEM) search concentrates its proposal. We analyze sufficient conditions under which latent distance preserves real-cost rankings, identifying encoder distortion, terminal rollout error, and candidate margins as the controlling quantities. Guided by the observed empirical alignment gap, DA-LeWM augments LeWM with inverse-dynamics and demonstration-conditioned goal-action heads. Across all our experiments, DA-LeWM accelerates convergence and achieves higher online success than LeWM, while probe scores remain similar. These results show that action-conditioned objectives improve the geometry used by Euclidean-cost, CEM-based latent MPC.

</details>


### [52] [Geometric Data Perturbation with Noisy-Anchor Alignment for Privacy-Preserving Collaborative Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18749)
*Keiyu Nosaka,Yamato Suetake,Yuichi Takano,Yukihiko Okada,Akiko Yoshise*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为锚点噪声的新方法，用于在分析师与参与者合谋的场景下保护GDP隐私协作学习，相比传统的私有数据噪声方法，在相同隐私保护水平下实现了更高的模型准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的GDP方法在分析师与参与者合谋攻击下容易泄露非合谋参与者的私有数据。虽然参与者特定的独立变换可以抵抗这种攻击，但会导致数据表示空间不兼容从而降低模型性能。现有的数据协作（DC）方法通过共享锚点矩阵恢复兼容性，但披露锚点矩阵反而使非合谋参与者的私有数据面临被精确恢复的风险。

Method: 提出锚点噪声方法：每个参与者独立地变换其私有数据和共享锚点矩阵，仅对锚点表示添加噪声，然后在单轮中将两种表示上传。分析师通过求解广义正交普氏问题，利用含噪锚点表示来对齐私有数据表示。作者刻画了对齐和恢复误差，分析了收敛的充分条件，并研究了三种恢复攻击。

Result: 在MNIST和CelebA数据集上的实验表明，在评估的攻击和部署设置下，锚点噪声方法在相同测量泄漏水平下比私有数据噪声方法实现了更高的学习准确率，在指定合谋模型下实现了更优的隐私-效用权衡。

Conclusion: 所提出的锚点噪声方法成功解决了GDP在合谋攻击下的隐私泄露问题，同时保持了良好的模型效用，为隐私保护协作学习提供了一个更优的解决方案。

Abstract: Geometric Data Perturbation (GDP) enables one-shot, privacy-preserving collaborative learning: each participant applies a distance-preserving transformation to its private data and uploads only the resulting representation to a central analyst. We study GDP under analyst-participant collusion, in which the analyst combines all uploaded representations with the private data and transformations disclosed by colluding participants to recover a non-colluding participant's private data. Participant-specific independent transformations resist this attack but map participants' data into incompatible representation spaces, degrading downstream model performance. Shared-anchor alignment from Data Collaboration (DC) analysis restores compatibility and improves utility, but we show that disclosing the DC anchor matrix enables exact recovery of non-colluding participants' private data even in the presence of collusion. Adding noise directly to the private-data representations mitigates this vulnerability but substantially reduces utility. We propose adding noise to the anchor representations instead. Each participant independently transforms its private data and the shared anchor matrix, perturbs only the resulting anchor representation, and uploads both representations in a single round. Using the noisy anchor representations, the analyst aligns the private-data representations by solving a Generalized Orthogonal Procrustes Problem. We characterize alignment and recovery errors, specialize a conservative sufficient condition for convergence of the alignment to our setting, and analyze three recovery attacks. Experiments on MNIST and CelebA show that, across the evaluated attacks and deployment settings, anchor noise achieves higher learning accuracy than private-data noise at comparable measured leakage, yielding a more favorable privacy-utility trade-off under the specified collusion model.

</details>


### [53] [Beyond Predictive Fairness: Quantifying Attribution Consistency Across Demographic Groups in Diabetic Retinopathy Screening](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18759)
*Kerol Djoumessi,Philipp Berens*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个基于Jensen-Shannon散度的公平性感知指标ECS，用于量化不同亚群间归因图的相似性。研究发现预测性能的种族差异与解释一致性之间无显著关联，表明预测公平性与解释一致性捕捉了模型行为的互补维度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学影像公平性通常通过亚群性能指标评估，但模型是否在不同人口群体间依赖一致的视觉证据尚不清楚。现有评估方法未能揭示模型决策依据在群体间的差异，需要一个新的指标来衡量跨群体的解释一致性。

Method: 引入解释一致性分数(ECS)，一个基于Jensen-Shannon散度的公平性感知指标，用于量化跨亚群归因图的相似性。以糖尿病视网膜病变筛查为案例研究，在全球范围内和疾病严重程度内评估ECS，分析预测性能差异与解释一致性之间的关联。

Result: 实验表明，虽然预测性能在不同族裔群体间存在差异，但解释一致性相对较高，且与性能差异无显著关联。预测性能较差的群体与性能较好群体获得的视觉证据解释是相似的。

Conclusion: 预测公平性和解释一致性捕捉了模型行为的互补维度，这促使公平性评估应超越单纯的预测性能指标。研究为医学影像公平性评估提供了新的视角，强调需要同时关注模型的预测结果和决策依据的一致性。

Abstract: Fairness in medical imaging is commonly evaluated through subgroup performance metrics, yet it remains unclear whether models rely on consistent visual evidence across demographic groups. This work introduces the Explanation Consistency Score (ECS), a fairness-aware metric based on Jensen-Shannon divergence that quantifies the similarity of attribution maps across subgroups. Using diabetic retinopathy screening as a case study, ECS is evaluated globally and within disease severity. Experiments reveal that while predictive performance differs across ethnic groups, explanation consistency remains relatively high and shows no significant association with performance disparities. These findings suggest that predictive fairness and explanation consistency capture complementary dimensions of model behavior, motivating fairness evaluations that extend beyond predictive performance.

</details>


### [54] [Enhancing Distance-Based Graph Autoencoders with Structural Penalties for Dynamic Graph Embedding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18762)
*Aleksandar Tomčić,Miloš Savić,Miloš Radovanović*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了两种基于距离的图自编码器变体，通过引入基于度中心性和自然社区局部固有维度的节点级正则化项，有效提升了动态图重建性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图自编码器的优化目标通常未考虑节点间的结构异质性，且先前研究表明高NC-LID值与嵌入质量下降相关，这启发了作者设计能够强调结构模糊节点重建误差的方法。

Method: 采用双层图卷积网络编码器和欧氏距离解码器，在稀疏校正损失基础上扩展了两个节点级正则化项：(i)基于度中心性的枢纽惩罚项；(ii)基于自然社区局部固有维度(NC-LID)的惩罚项，从而将结构惩罚融入重建损失中。

Result: 在多个动态图数据集上的实验表明，融入NC-LID正则化的方法在重建性能上持续优于无结构正则化的基线方法和基于枢纽感知正则化的方法，验证了NC-LID作为结构信号的有效性。

Conclusion: NC-LID是增强动态环境下基于距离的图自编码器的有效结构信号，为处理结构异质性提供了新思路。

Abstract: Graph autoencoders (GAEs) are widely used for learning representations of dynamic graphs. However, their optimisation objectives typically do not take structural heterogeneity across nodes into account. We propose three distance-based GAE variants that incorporate structural penalties into the reconstruction loss. All variants share a two-layer Graph Convolutional Network encoder and a Euclidean-distance decoder trained with distance-based reconstruction objectives. We extend sparsity-corrected loss with two node-level regularization terms: (i) a hub penalty based on degree centrality, and (ii) a penalty based on Natural Community Local Intrinsic Dimensionality (NC-LID). The paper is motivated by prior evidence linking high NC-LID to reduced embedding quality. The proposed methods are designed to emphasize reconstruction errors for structurally ambiguous nodes. Experiments on multiple dynamic graph data sets show that incorporating NC-LID-based regularization consistently improves reconstruction performance over the baseline without structural regularization and the method using hub-aware regularization. These findings highlight NC-LID as a useful structural signal for enhancing distance-based graph autoencoders in dynamic settings.

</details>


### [55] [To Go Far, Go Together: Diverse Preferences Induce a Curriculum for Reward Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18770)
*Taehyung Kim,Jongeun Choi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出CurriPO方法，通过构建树状课程学习解决多用户AI对齐中的优化不公平问题，显著提升弱势用户群体的满意度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于人类反馈的AI对齐方法关注奖励模型的数据效率和准确性，但忽视了优化层面的公平性：不同用户的奖励模型优化难度不同，优化困难的用户成为新的弱势群体。

Method: 提出CurriPO方法，自动构建树状课程学习结构覆盖多样化用户奖励模型。该算法根据优化难度对用户排序，允许从现有课程分支并复用已纳入的奖励模型，实现对多用户目标的系统性覆盖。

Result: 在个性化连续控制模拟环境中，CurriPO在人群满意度上达到最强基线1.2-2.1倍的性能，同时大幅缩短训练时间。分析表明性能提升主要来自传统优化方法未能有效服务的用户群体。

Conclusion: 多用户奖励模型之间存在自然的学习难度梯度，利用这种结构构建课程学习可有效解决优化层面的公平性问题。这是首个显式利用多用户结构解决AI对齐优化问题的工作。

Abstract: Learning a reward model from human feedback and optimizing a policy against it is one approach to aligning AI systems with individual users. From a fairness perspective, existing work improves such alignment by developing data-efficient and accurate reward models that capture minority preferences despite scarce data. We push this line of inquiry one step further and argue that data-efficient and accurate per-user reward models are not sufficient: users whose reward models are difficult to \textit{optimize} at the policy level can become a new underserved group. We start from the observation that one user's reward model can be easy to optimize from the initial policy while another's is not. We argue that, given a sufficiently diverse user population, a curriculum naturally emerges between easy- and hard-to-optimize reward models. Building on this insight, we propose CurriPO, which grows a tree-structured curriculum to accommodate diverse user-specific objectives, covering the population in a single traversal. Specifically, CurriPO automatically constructs a curriculum over diverse user reward models, allowing it to branch from the existing curriculum and reuse reward models previously incorporated into the curriculum. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first work to explicitly exploit multi-user structure to address optimization in AI alignment. Extensive experiments on personalized continuous control in a simulated environment show that CurriPO achieves $1.2$--$2.1\times$ the population satisfaction of the strongest baseline while substantially reducing training time. Additional analysis attributes much of this improvement to the users left underserved by conventional optimization.

</details>


### [56] [GraphK: Variable-Size Graph Generation with Efficient Edge Construction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18777)
*Resul Tugay,Eren Oluğ,Elif Ak,Sule Gunduz Oguducu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了GraphK，一种新颖的编码器-采样器-解码器图生成框架。它通过学习置换不变的潜在表示和基于KD树的边预测方法，解决了现有模型在可扩展性、灵活性方面的限制，实现了图大小的灵活控制（包括放大和缩小），并在实验中超越了现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的深度学习图生成模型在可扩展性、灵活性和建模潜在结构能力方面仍然存在局限。具体来说，自回归方法受限于词汇表大小（即图生成中的节点数量），难以灵活控制输出图的大小，且计算成本较高。

Method: 提出GraphK框架，采用编码器-采样器-解码器架构。核心方法包括：1) 学习置换不变的潜在表示；2) 通过最大似然估计采样新节点嵌入，实现对图大小和结构的泛化；3) 使用基于KD树的top-k邻居搜索进行边预测，降低计算成本；4) 基于流形平滑假设捕获图属性。

Result: 在合成数据集和真实世界数据集上的实验表明，GraphK在多项指标上优于现有方法。该方法能够准确学习图结构，生成无需显式定义的合成图，同时支持图大小的灵活控制（包括生成比输入图更多或更少节点的图）。

Conclusion: GraphK成功克服了现有图生成模型的关键限制，通过结构灵活性和计算效率实现了高质量的图生成。该方法为图生成领域提供了一种可扩展、灵活且高效的解决方案，能够有效捕捉图的潜在结构特性。

Abstract: Graph generation models have advanced significantly with deep learning, yet they remain limited in scalability, flexibility, and ability to model underlying structures. We present GraphK, a novel encoder-sampler-decoder framework for graph generation that overcomes these challenges through structural flexibility and computational efficiency. Unlike autoregressive approaches constrained by vocabulary size (i.e. number of nodes in graph generation), GraphK allows for both upscaling (generating graphs with more nodes than the input) and downscaling, providing a flexible control over output graph size. By learning permutation-invariant latent representations and sampling new node embeddings via maximum likelihood estimation, GraphK generalizes across graph sizes and structures. For edge generation, we employ edge prediction with a KDTree-based top-k neighbor search in the latent space, reducing computational cost. Based on the manifold smoothness assumption, our method effectively captures graph properties. Experiments on synthetic and real-world datasets show that GraphK outperforms existing methods, accurately learns graph structures, and generates synthetic graphs without explicit definitions.

</details>


### [57] [A Real-Time Tsetlin Machine-based Non-intrusive Load Monitoring System on MCUs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18780)
*Tianhang Tan,Han Wu,Tousif Rahman,Shengyu Duan,Alex Yakovlev,Rishad Shafik*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于Tsetlin Machine的NILM框架，实现了在资源受限的微控制器上的实时、隐私保护的负载监测，模型仅占用18 KB闪存，在ESP32上推理延迟0.43 ms。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统NILM系统使用计算密集型优化算法处理离线数据，限制了其在需要本地处理敏感家庭数据的边缘设备上的部署能力，缺乏适合微控制器的轻量级实时解决方案。

Method: 将NILM问题重构为分类任务，采用Tsetlin Machine构建框架，针对资源受限的微控制器优化，实现隐私保护的边缘部署，在REDD数据集上进行验证。

Result: 在双电器分类任务上平均精度90%、召回率96%；四电器分类精度77%、召回率80%。训练模型仅占用18 KB闪存，在ESP32上推理延迟0.43 ms。

Conclusion: 该方法成功实现了在嵌入式微控制器上的实时NILM应用，证明了Tsetlin Machine在资源受限环境下进行高效、隐私保护的负载监测的可行性和优越性。

Abstract: Non-Intrusive Load Monitoring (NILM) systems estimate individual appliance energy consumption from a single aggregate meter, without requiring separate sensors for each device. By installing a single meter that measures a building's total electricity consumption, NILM algorithms can determine the active status of each appliance. However, traditional NILM systems use computationally intensive optimization algorithms to process offline data, limiting their capability for on-device deployment, where sensitive household data must be processed locally. This paper proposes a Tsetlin Machine (TM)-based NILM framework, targeting real-time applications on resource-constrained microcontrollers (MCUs), enabling privacy-preserving edge deployment. The problem is reformulated as a classification task, and the proposed approach achieves an average precision of 90% and recall of 96% for two-appliance classification, and 77% precision and 80% recall for four appliances on the REDD dataset. The trained model occupies only 18 KB of flash memory and achieves an inference latency of 0.43 ms on an ESP32, demonstrating its suitability for embedded NILM applications on MCUs.

</details>


### [58] [Forgetting, plasticity, and co-observation: a third facet of continual learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18803)
*Timm Hess,Abhishek Jha,Gido M. van de Ven,Tinne Tuytelaars*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了持续学习中的第三个关键因素——数据协同观察，它独立于灾难性遗忘和可塑性损失，对模型泛化能力有重要影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持续学习面临的主要挑战通常被认为是灾难性遗忘和可塑性丧失，但这两个问题无法完全解释朴素序列训练与离线联合训练之间的性能差距。研究者希望探索是否存在其他被忽视的因素影响持续学习性能。

Method: 通过解耦数据访问限制与稳定性、可塑性的约束，系统地研究了同时观察训练数据带来的表征优势。在监督学习和自监督学习范式下，使用通用的数据增量"分块"场景，对比联合训练和分离训练的性能差异，同时控制遗忘和可塑性因素。

Result: 实验表明，在控制遗忘和可塑性的条件下，联合训练与分离训练之间存在一致的性能差异。同时观察训练数据（协同观察）带来的泛化优势远超知识保留的范畴，且该效应不需要特定的持续分布偏移。蒸馏方法仅作为知识保留机制，而记忆重放不仅缓解遗忘，还主动将数据协同观察的优势重新引入学习过程。

Conclusion: 数据协同观察是影响持续学习性能的独特因素，其收益超越了简单的知识保留。这一发现重新定义了对持续学习机制的理解，为设计更有效的持续学习方法提供了新视角。

Abstract: Efficient continual learning remains a fundamental challenge for deep neural networks. While catastrophic forgetting and loss of plasticity are widely considered the primary obstacles to overcome, we show that these two issues cannot fully explain the performance gap between naive sequential training and offline joint training. In this paper, we highlight data co-observation as a distinct factor influencing continual learning performance. By decoupling the constraints of separate data access from stability and plasticity, we systematically investigate the representational benefits gained by observing training data together. Empirically, we demonstrate a consistent performance difference between joint and separate training across both supervised and self-supervised paradigms in generic data-incremental "chunking" scenarios, whilst mitigating forgetting and controlling for plasticity. Our findings indicate that simultaneous observation of training data (co-observation) yields benefits to the learner's generalization that extend well beyond mere knowledge retention, and that this effect does not require a specific continual distribution shift. Furthermore, we contextualize prominent continual learning mechanisms through this lens: while distillation-based approaches act only as effective knowledge retention mechanisms, our results suggest that the empirical success of memory replay goes beyond the mitigation of forgetting, actively reintroducing the benefits of data co-observation into the learning process.

</details>


### [59] [Tensor Field Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18808)
*Alexander Strunk,Roland Assam*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了张量场模型(TFMs)，一种实现层面的数学结构，通过可复用的条件表示提升了生成性能并加速了采样过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的生成模型缺乏对条件信息的高效利用机制，且分析性和动态性约束通常在定义层面施加限制。本文旨在构建一种数学结构，使得这些约束通过可容许族的选择进行编码，同时实现条件表示的复用，从而加速生成过程。

Method: 提出张量场模型(TFMs)，其中学习算子将可容许的组件-截面族的乘积映射到生成状态流形上预定的依赖时间的切截面族。构建了构造型、组件可分离型和张量丛型TFM三种结构化细化方案。在条件实现中，结构化条件被逐组件映射为可复用的集合，组件表示保持独立，仅通过场算子组合生成向量场。所有模型使用流匹配(Flow Matching)方法训练。

Result: 实验表明TFMs能够提升生成性能，且通过可复用条件表示实现的摊销采样能够加速生成过程。

Conclusion: TFMs提供了一个统一且灵活的数学框架，通过结构化的条件表示复用机制，在保持生成质量的同时显著提升了采样效率，为条件生成任务提供了一种有效的解决方案。

Abstract: This paper introduces Tensor Field Models (TFMs), realization-level Mathematical Structures in which a learned Operator maps a product of admissible component-section families to a prescribed family of time-dependent tangent sections on a Generative State Manifold. Analytic and dynamical restrictions are encoded through the choice of admissible families rather than imposed by the root definition. Constructed, component-separable, and Tensor Bundle TFMs provide structured refinements of this common object. In the conditional realizations considered here, a structured condition $c=(c_1,\ldots,c_n)$ is mapped componentwise to a reusable collection $\mathbf H_c=(H_{c_1}^{(1)},\ldots,H_{c_n}^{(n)})$. In the architectures evaluated here, the component representations remain distinct and are combined only by the Field Operator to produce the generated Vector Field. All learned models are trained using Flow Matching. Experiments show that TFMs can improve performance and that amortized sampling enabled by reusable condition representations can accelerate generation.

</details>


### [60] [Many Optimizers But Only One Training Path: Repeated Resampling for Adaptive Optimizer Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18810)
*Ronald Richman,Mario V. Wüthrich*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种在训练过程中动态选择优化器的方法（ROR），仅需最佳固定优化器24%-35%的总训练量即可达到相近性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统方法在训练前固定优化器，将其作为超参数调优需要多次完整训练且浪费计算资源；需要一种更高效的方法来动态选择优化器。

Method: 重复优化器重采样（ROR）：在单个训练过程中，每隔b个epoch让候选优化器从当前权重"侦察"s个epoch，选择最佳侦察结果继续训练剩余b-s个epoch；允许优化器偏好随训练进程动态变化。

Result: 在MNIST、Fashion-MNIST和两个机动车辆保险索赔计数模型上测试，单epoch ROR仅需穷举搜索最佳固定优化器总训练量的24%-35%，且在所有四个任务上性能接近最佳固定优化器。

Conclusion: 短期侦察（short scouting）是一种实用的优化器搜索方法，无需完成所有候选优化器的完整训练即可实现有效选择。

Abstract: An optimizer is usually chosen before training a deep neural network and then kept fixed. Treating optimizer choice as a hyperparameter could boost performance, but it requires several complete training runs and discards all but the winner. Repeated Optimizer Resampling (ROR) instead searches during one evolving run. Every $b$ epochs, each candidate optimizer scouts from the current model weights for $s$ epochs. The best scout continues for the remaining $b-s$ epochs, and that completed segment becomes the new incumbent if it improves the validation objective. This design allows the preferred optimizer to change as training progresses.
  We compare two variants of ROR on MNIST, Fashion-MNIST, and two motor insurance claim-count models. Nine fixed optimizers and both ROR variants are evaluated with the same ten seeds. One-epoch ROR uses 24\% to 35\% of the aggregate training needed to identify the best fixed optimizer exhaustively and remains close to that optimizer on all four tasks. These results support short scouting as a practical way to search over optimizers without completing every candidate run.

</details>


### [61] [A Unifying Relational Perspective on Expressive Lottery Tickets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18819)
*Lorenz Kummer,Samir Moustafa,Anatol Ehrlich,Franka Bause,Marco Nennstiel,Przemysław Andrzej Wałȩga,Nils Morten Kriege*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文扩展了强表达彩票假设（SELTH）至关系图神经网络（RGNNs）和时序图神经网络（TGNNs），证明了充分参数化的RGNNs包含能保持1-RWL表达能力的稀疏子网络，并给出了随机剪枝获得此类子网络的概率下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图神经网络（GNNs）广泛应用，但参数稀疏性如何影响关系图神经网络（RGNNs）和时序图神经网络（TGNNs）的表达能力尚不清楚。现有强表达彩票假设（SELTH）仅针对静态图，缺乏对多关系和时序领域的理论理解。

Method: 1）将SELTH存在性结果推广至多关系和时序领域的概率性表述，基于关系WL测试（RWL）；2）证明充分参数化的RGNNs存在保持1-RWL表达能力的稀疏子网络；3）推导随机剪枝获得此类子网络的概率下界；4）展示常见TGNNs和跨图消息传递方案可重构为RGNN形式以继承这些保证；5）建立稀疏RGNN表达能力与优化行为的理论联系。

Result: 证明了RGNNs中存在保持1-RWL表达能力的稀疏子网络，推导了随机剪枝成功概率的理论下界，并建立了稀疏RGNN表达能力与优化行为的理论联系。实验在合成数据上验证了理论界，并在时序和分子基准数据集上研究了预训练表达能力与优化和预测质量指标的关系。

Conclusion: 成功将强表达彩票假设从静态图推广至关系和时序图神经网络领域，为参数稀疏化在保持表达能力方面提供了理论保证，揭示了稀疏性与优化行为的内在联系，为GNN模型压缩和架构设计提供了理论指导。

Abstract: Graph neural networks (GNNs) are widely used, but how parameter sparsity affects the expressivity of relational (RGNNs) and temporal (TGNNs) variants is poorly understood. The Strong Expressive Lottery Ticket Hypothesis (SELTH) posits the existence of sparse GNNs that preserve Weisfeiler-Leman (WL) expressivity on static graphs. We generalize this existence result to a probabilistic statement for multi-relational and temporal domains via the relational WL (RWL). We prove that sufficiently parameterized RGNNs contain sparse subnetworks that maintain 1-RWL expressivity and derive a lower bound on the probability that a random pruning yields such a subnetwork. We show that common TGNNs and cross-graph message passing schemes admit RGNN reformulations such that they inherit these guarantees and, moreover, that the expressivity of a sparse RGNN is connected to its optimization behavior under common update regimes. Experiments instantiate the bound, compare it to empirical probabilities on synthetic data, and study how pre-training expressivity relates to optimization and prediction quality metrics on temporal and molecular benchmarks.

</details>


### [62] [MLREF: Efficient Module Reuse for Reward Design in Reinforcement Learning via Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18827)
*Chenglin Liu,Xun Wang,Ruishuo Chen,Zhuoran Li,Longbo Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MLREF框架，通过模块化的奖励函数演化机制，解决了现有LLM自动化奖励生成方法中难以保留和复用有效组件的问题，在17个任务上实现了显著性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 奖励函数设计仍是强化学习的瓶颈。虽然大语言模型（LLMs）已实现自动化奖励生成，但现有方法将奖励函数作为整体程序进行生成和修订，难以可靠地保留和复用早期迭代中发现的有效组件，导致跨迭代性能不稳定。

Method: 提出模块级奖励演化框架（MLREF）。核心是模块池——一个可复用奖励组件的持久化存储库。MLREF将模块池作为主要优化对象：通过积累成功模块、优化表现不佳的模块、复用已验证组件来驱动池的演化；奖励函数则构造为从池中抽取的模块的线性组合。框架整合三种机制：基于反思的优化、混合信用分配、带回滚的合并策略，共同提升奖励优化的有效性和鲁棒性。

Result: 在17个任务上的实验表明，MLREF在运动任务上超越强基线25.2%，在操作任务上提升6.6%，且优化动力学更加稳定。

Conclusion: MLREF通过模块化的奖励函数演化方法，有效解决了奖励函数设计中组件复用和性能稳定性问题，为强化学习中的自动化奖励设计提供了更可靠和高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Reward function design remains a bottleneck in reinforcement learning. While large language models (LLMs) have enabled automated reward generation, existing methods generate and revise reward functions as monolithic programs, making it difficult to reliably preserve and reuse effective components discovered in earlier iterations, leading to unstable performance across iterations. To address this, we propose Module Level Reward Evolution Framework (MLREF). At the core of MLREF is a module pool, a persistent repository of reusable reward components. MLREF treats the module pool as the primary optimization object: the pool evolves across iterations by accumulating successful modules, refining underperforming ones, and reusing proven components; while reward functions are constructed as linear combinations of modules drawn from this pool. To drive this evolution, MLREF integrates three mechanisms: reflection-based refinement, hybrid credit assignment, and a merge strategy with rollback, which together improve the effectiveness and robustness of reward optimization. Experiments on 17 tasks show that MLREF outperforms strong baselines by 25.2% in locomotion and 6.6% in manipulation, with more stable optimization dynamics.

</details>


### [63] [GEAR: Generative Expansion and Real Anchoring for Two-Stage Distillation of Tabular Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18849)
*Qi Qin,Jiajie Zhu,Dali Chen,Yuzhao Zhang,Jia-Xing Han,Yu Su,Peng Zhang,Ying Yan,Yifan Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出GEAR框架，通过两阶段蒸馏将表格基础模型转化为轻量级MLP或树模型，显著提升性能的同时大幅降低推理延迟和内存占用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表格基础模型通过上下文学习实现了优异性能，但其依赖上下文的推理机制带来了显著的延迟和内存开销，阻碍了大规模部署。需要一种方法在保持高性能的同时降低推理成本。

Method: 提出GEAR两阶段框架：第一阶段使用合成协变量作为教师查询位置，在软TFM目标上训练学生模型，扩展覆盖范围；第二阶段使用真实标签和袋外教师预测重新锚定学生模型到目标分布，避免自标注泄露。并推导了生成查询量与生成器保真度之间权衡的风险证书。

Result: 在TALENT和TabArena上验证：两阶段MLP相比监督MLP在二分类任务提升1.81-2.00 AUC，多分类提升1.19-1.35点；相比仅真实数据蒸馏额外提升1.76-2.19和2.09-2.40点。三个学生模型家族在二分类任务上均超越CatBoost。推理时间减少57-2866倍，预测内存峰值降低1.9-3.3倍。

Conclusion: GEAR成功将TFM蒸馏为轻量级预测器，在保持更高AUC的同时显著降低了推理成本，实现了性能与效率的有效平衡，为TFM的实际部署提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Tabular foundation models (TFMs) achieve strong performance through in-context learning, but context-dependent inference imposes substantial latency and memory costs, hindering large-scale deployment. We propose GEAR (\emph{Generative Expansion and Real Anchoring}), a modular two-stage framework that distills TFMs into lightweight MLP or tree-based predictors that can be deployed on commodity CPUs. Stage 1 uses synthetic covariates solely as teacher-query locations and trains the student on soft TFM targets, expanding coverage beyond observed rows. Stage 2 re-anchors the student to the target distribution using real labels and out-of-fold teacher predictions, whitch avoids self-labeling leakage. We further derive a risk certificate characterizing the trade-off between generated-query volume and generator fidelity. Experiments on TALENT and TabArena demonstrate the broad applicability of GEAR. Two-stage MLPs outperform supervised MLPs by 1.81--2.00 AUC points on binary tasks and 1.19--1.35 points on multiclass tasks, with additional gains over real-data-only distillation of 1.76--2.19 and 2.09--2.40 points, respectively. On binary tasks, the gains also transfer to LightGBM and XGBoost, and all three student families outperform CatBoost, the strongest non-TFM baseline, in mean AUC. Ablations show gains beyond longer training or alternative warm starts, greater stability from staged than mixed optimization, and generator-dependent diminishing returns as query volume increases. Finally, GEAR reduces median inference time by 57--2866 times and peak prediction memory by 1.9--3.3 times, while retaining higher AUC than matched supervised baselines.

</details>


### [64] [Multi-stage neural operator learning with application for convolutions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18851)
*Zhiping Mao,Zhenye Wen,Yong Zhang,Xiaofei Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了两种多阶段神经算子学习框架（DCNO和DGNO），用于快速准确计算卷积积分，在单精度浮点数下可达到接近机器精度的准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 卷积积分在应用中广泛存在，为了实现快速且准确的计算，需要开发高效的算子学习方法来替代传统求解器，特别是在需要大量查询或参数变化的场景下。

Method: 提出两种框架：1）Deep Collocation Neural Operator (DCNO)是有监督方法，通过迭代学习输入-输出数据对的残差来精细化算子近似；2）Deep Galerkin Neural Operator (DGNO)是无监督框架，当目标算子可用PDE表示时，利用PDE残差的弱形式进行训练。两种方法都通过多个训练阶段逐步构建基算子来丰富近似空间。

Result: 两种方法都提供了理论分析证明其近似能力；在卷积问题中，单精度浮点数下可达到接近机器精度的高准确性；与传统求解器相比，在大量查询或参数变化情况下提供显著的效率提升；框架可扩展到多输入算子学习场景（涉及卷积密度和核的变化）。

Conclusion: 所提出的多阶段神经算子学习框架显著优于标准一次性算子学习方法，为卷积积分计算提供了高效准确的解决方案，具有良好的理论保证和实际应用价值。

Abstract: Convolution integrals widely exist in applications, and to enable fast and accurate computations, this paper introduces two general multi-stage neural operator learning frameworks. The first, Deep Collocation Neural Operator (DCNO), is a supervised approach that iteratively refines the operator approximation by learning residuals from input-output data pairs. The second, Deep Galerkin Neural Operator (DGNO), is an unsupervised framework applicable when the target operator can be represented by a PDE, leveraging the weak form of the PDE residual for training. Both methods progressively construct basis operators through multiple training stages to enrich the approximation space, leading to significantly improved accuracy over standard one-shot operator learning. We provide theoretical analysis for their approximation capabilities and implement them for learning convolutions. Extensive numerical experiments demonstrate that both DCNO and DGNO achieve high accuracy, approaching machine precision under single float for convolution problems, and offer substantial efficiency gains for numerous queries or parametric variations compared to traditional solvers. We also extend these frameworks to handle multi-input operator learning scenarios involving variations in both the density and kernel of a convolution.

</details>


### [65] [Graph-Based Approaches to Learning Epileptogenic Zone Localization Using Stereo-EEG Recordings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18887)
*Daniel Wendelken,Brian Ervin,Ravindra Arya,Ali A. Minai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究系统比较了不同图拓扑结构对静息态sEEG致痫区定位的影响，发现提出的Region-Bridge-c方法在约30%边保留率下达到最优性能（PR-AUC 0.371），比全连接图减少69%的边且性能更优，表明图构建应作为显式评估环节而非固定预处理步骤。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 致痫区(EZ)定位是癫痫手术的关键目标，但人工解读sEEG记录耗时且仅关注发作期数据。基于静息态功能连接的图学习模型是可行替代方案，然而模型性能关键依赖于网络拓扑结构的选择，现有研究缺乏对不同图构建方法的系统比较。

Method: 在40名患者数据上采用相同可学习模型和留一患者交叉验证，控制每个节点入边数量并变化图稀疏度，系统比较了稠密图、解剖/几何先验、预算稀疏化方法、学习稀疏化以及提出的Region-Bridge-c拓扑等多种图构建方法。

Result: Region-Bridge-c在约30%边保留率下取得最高平均PR-AUC (0.371±0.015; ROC-AUC 0.743±0.010)，相比稠密图(PR-AUC 0.349)减少69%边数；Spatial-k表现相近，随机剪枝需要接近稠密保留率；学习稀疏化虽受益于解剖元数据，但平均性能未超越最优固定先验；各拓扑的最佳选择因患者而异。

Conclusion: 图构建方法对EZ定位性能有显著影响，不应视为固定预处理步骤而应显式评估比较；Region-Bridge-c拓扑能在较少边数下达到更优性能，但个体化选择仍是关键；研究为临床sEEG分析中的图拓扑选择提供了实证指导。

Abstract: The epileptogenic zone (EZ) is the brain region that generates seizures in an individual, and is the target of epilepsy surgery. Localizing the EZ from stereo-EEG (sEEG) recordings supports surgical planning, but manual interpretation is time-consuming and focuses on seizure recordings. Graphical learning models of resting-state functional connectivity among the recorded brain regions are an attractive alternative, but depend crucially on the network topology chosen for the model.
  We present a controlled study of graph-based models to explore how graph topology affects EZ localization from resting-state sEEG in 40 patients. Using the same simple learnable model and leave-one-patient-out evaluation, we compare dense graphs, anatomy- and geometry-informed priors, budgeted sparsification methods, and learned sparsification, including the proposed Region-Bridge-$c$ topology. To compare graph constructions fairly, we control the number of incoming edges per node and vary graph sparsity.
  At $\approx 30\%$ edge retention, Region-Bridge-$c$ achieves the highest observed mean PR-AUC ($0.371\pm0.015$; ROC-AUC $0.743\pm0.010$) while using $\approx 69\%$ fewer edges than Dense (PR-AUC $0.349\pm0.014$). Spatial-$k$ is competitive, whereas random pruning requires near-dense retention. Learned sparsification benefits from anatomical node metadata but, on average, does not surpass the best fixed prior. Across all topologies, the best choice varies by patient. These results suggest that graph construction should be evaluated explicitly rather than treated as fixed preprocessing.

</details>


### [66] [A FEM-Based Surrogate Modelling and Optimization Framework for Physics-Constrained Electromagnetic Coil Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18903)
*Yucheng Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对比了三种优化算法在电磁线圈-铁芯设计基准问题上的性能表现，发现不同算法在不同预算阶段表现各异：贝叶斯优化在低预算下改进迅速，COBYLA在早期占优，而BOBYQA获得最终最佳结果。研究表明在仿真驱动设计中，不同评价指标可能偏好不同优化方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在仿真驱动的工程设计中，计算代价高昂的有限元模型需要高效的优化策略。代理辅助优化（如高斯过程代理模型结合贝叶斯优化）有望降低计算成本，但其相对于传统无导数优化方法的性能优势尚不清楚，特别是在受多种物理约束的实际设计问题中。本研究旨在系统性评估不同优化方法在物理约束条件下的性能表现。

Method: 建立了Python-MPh-COMSOL工作流程，将二维轴对称有限元模型与Matern 5/2高斯过程代理模型耦合。采用序列贝叶斯优化（BO）配合期望改进（EI）获取函数进行候选排序。针对七参数电流激励线圈-铁芯设计问题，考虑几何约束、制造约束以及独立的铁芯和铜质量约束。每组优化运行均通过FEM验证代理模型的预测结果。对比了三种方法：EI-BO、COBYLA和BOBYQA。

Result: 五组配对实验显示：优化器排名依赖于可用的FEM评估预算——EI-BO在小预算下快速改进，COBYLA在最早检查点表现更强，BOBYQA获得最高的平均终端响应值。事后有限池研究发现，在该光滑响应面上，EI排序相对于后验均值排序并无稳健的终点优势。在固定总电流条件下验证设计时，BOBYQA-COBYLA-EI-BO的性能排序保持不变。

Conclusion: 在仿真驱动设计中，早期进展、终端响应、信息利用率和计算时间成本可能偏好不同的优化方法，不存在普适最优的优化策略。然而，该结论仅适用于此轴对称基准问题，并未建立固定电流最优解、固定功率性能或电气效率优越性的普适性结论。研究强调了针对具体设计问题选择优化方法的重要性。

Abstract: This work evaluates surrogate-assisted optimization of a seven-parameter current-excited coil--core benchmark subject to geometric, manufacturing, and separate core and copper mass constraints. A Python--MPh--COMSOL workflow couples a two-dimensional axisymmetric finite-element method (FEM) model to a Matern 5/2 Gaussian-process (GP) probabilistic surrogate. Here, physics-constrained denotes a design problem evaluated by a governing-equation FEM model and restricted by explicit physical, geometric, manufacturing, and material-allocation constraints; it does not denote a physics-informed GP architecture. Sequential Bayesian optimization (BO) ranks candidates using expected improvement (EI), and every reported incumbent is verified by FEM. Five paired runs show that optimizer ranking depends on the available FEM-evaluation budget: EI--BO improves rapidly at small continuation budgets, COBYLA is stronger at the earliest checkpoint, and BOBYQA attains the highest mean terminal response. A retrospective finite-pool study further finds no robust endpoint advantage of EI over posterior-mean ranking on this smooth response surface. The broader result is that early progress, terminal response, information use, and wall-clock cost can favor different methods in simulation-driven design. A selected-design check at a common total current preserves the observed BOBYQA--COBYLA--EI-BO ordering. The conclusions nevertheless remain conditional on this axisymmetric benchmark and do not establish a fixed-current optimum, fixed-power performance, or electrical-efficiency superiority.

</details>


### [67] [On the Slow Convergence to Trivial Solutions of Algorithms for Hard Optimization Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18910)
*Ali Hussaini Umar,Jean Barbier,Matthieu Jonckheere,Manuel Sáenz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了组合优化算法的有限尺寸行为，发现算法在中间约束密度区域的表现显著优于渐近理论预测，收敛速度缓慢，表明渐近失败预测并不否定精细算法设计的实践价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有平均情况分析主要关注渐近极限下的负面结果（高约束密度下多项式时间算法无法显著超越朴素启发式），但实际问题的有限尺寸行为可能揭示算法在渐近失败之前的非平凡表现，这一角度尚未得到充分探索。

Method: 结合大图渐近的严格分析与典型NP难问题（最大独立集、最大K-SAT）的数值实验，系统研究优化算法在易解、中间和困难三个区域中的有限尺寸行为与收敛特性。

Result: 算法最终确实收敛至理论预测界，但收敛极其缓慢；在中间约束密度区域（实例已高度受限），局部算法达到的解质量显著优于高约束密度渐近极限下的预测性能。

Conclusion: 有限尺寸行为与渐近行为之间存在显著差距，在实际应用中（即使渐近理论预言必然失败）复杂的算法设计仍然至关重要，不应仅依据渐近负面结果低估算法的实践潜力。

Abstract: Hard combinatorial optimization problems, many of which are NP-hard, present fundamental algorithmic challenges. Average-case analysis on random instances has emerged as a powerful framework for understanding typical algorithmic performance beyond worst-case guarantees. A substantial body of work has established negative results: for sufficiently hard instances (often controlled by the underlying graph connectivity/constraints density), no known polynomial-time algorithm can significantly outperform naive heuristics in the double asymptotic limit where both problem size and constraints density tend to infinity. We revisit this picture by studying the finite-size behavior of some optimization algorithms across easy, intermediate, and hard regimes. Through rigorous analysis of large-graph asymptotics combined with numerical experiments on canonical problems (maximum independent set and maximum $K$-SAT), we demonstrate that while algorithms do eventually converge to theoretically predicted bounds, this convergence can be remarkably slow. In the intermediate regime where instances are already highly constrained, local algorithms achieve solutions substantially better than their predicted performance in the high-constraint-density limit. This gap between finite-regime and asymptotic behavior has important practical implications: sophisticated algorithmic design remains crucial even when asymptotic theory predicts inevitable failure.

</details>


### [68] [Converting Expert Deliberation into Financial Signals Through A Context-Aware NLP Pipeline](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18911)
*Vivek Batra,Kristin Chen,Sanjiv Das,Samuel Judge,Harshad Khadilkar,Sukrit Mittal,Amir Nasrollahzadeh,Daniel Ostrov,Jacob Sisk*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CDSP框架，将投资委员会会议记录转化为结构化预测特征，用于预测下个月全球股票是否跑赢全球债券，最佳模型准确率达73%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索专家委员会讨论内容是否包含前瞻性信息，并研究如何通过上下文感知的自然语言处理技术从会议记录中提取有价值的预测信号。

Method: CDSP框架包含四个步骤：1）将会议记录分割为主题片段；2）使用大型语言模型分配资产类别上下文标签；3）将金融关键词映射到预定义的分类标签体系；4）构建情感极性和提及频率等互补特征。实验比较了工程特征、原始文本、句子嵌入和组合表示等多种方法。

Result: 使用48个月的委员会会议数据，预测准确率在62%-73%之间（基准策略"总是选择股票"的准确率为60.4%）。最佳模型结合句子嵌入和CDSP特征，准确率达73%，F1分数为0.73（但相比基准无统计显著性）。情感特征比提及频率携带更强的预测信号。

Conclusion: 专家讨论内容确实包含可通过上下文感知NLP提取的前瞻性信息，情感特征在某些分类类别中具有较强的预测价值，为从会议记录中挖掘投资信号提供了可行的方法论。

Abstract: We introduce the CDSP (context-conditional deliberation signal pipeline), converting an investment committee's meeting transcripts into structured predictive features. CDSP segments the meeting transcripts into topical chunks, assigns asset-class context labels using a large language model (LLM), maps financial keywords to a pre-determined taxonomy of labels, and constructs complementary features: sentiment polarity and mention frequency. This feature engineering framework is applied to a dataset spanning 48 monthly committee meetings to predict if global equities will perform better or worse than global bonds in the following month. In experiments with engineered features, raw transcript text, sentence embeddings, and combined representations, the prediction accuracy ranges from 62% to 73%, compared to always choosing stocks, which outperforms bonds 60.4% of the time. The best (73% accurate) model combines sentence embeddings with engineered CDSP features, achieving a 0.73 F1 score (although this is not statistically significant compared to always choosing stocks). Sentiment carries a stronger signal than mention frequency for several taxonomy categories. These findings suggest that experts' deliberations may contain forward-looking information that context-aware NLP can extract.

</details>


### [69] [Score the Algebra, Not the Span: Dimension Reduction for Transfer Operator Models of Dynamical Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18918)
*Mark Kozdoba,Shie Mannor*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对由弱相互作用组件组成的动力系统，传统基于谱模态的降维方法存在线性掩蔽问题（可能丢失整个组件）。本文提出基于σ-代数的评分方法，用χ²散度准则替代秩准则，以组件生成元成本代替所有交互成本，十几个代数坐标即可恢复传统秩方法在秩k<100时完全遗漏的组件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于秩的谱模态降维方法在处理由弱相互作用组件组成的动力系统时存在线性掩蔽问题：要么需要指数级数量的模态，要么完全丢弃某些组件（而非粗略建模），导致无法以任何精度预测该组件的任何函数。这是由于基于秩的模型每个模态需占用一个坐标造成的。

Method: 提出基于σ-代数的评分方法：不评估坐标本身，而是评估坐标生成的σ-代数，使得乘积和幂次免费，组件成本仅由其生成元而非所有交互决定。采用嵌入的现在与未来状态之间的χ²散度作为准则，具有预算保证：动力学内在维度的两倍坐标数足以实现嵌入，其代数可承载算子的整个谱。通过限制critic到双线性类，变分形式可用现成估计器，并回归到span上的VAMP分数。

Result: 在已发表基准系统复合测试中：基于秩的方法在所有秩k<100时完全遗漏掩蔽组件，而10个代数坐标即可恢复所有组件；代数表示支持从少量标签预测掩蔽组件，而从高维观测或VAMP特征的直接回归均失败。证明了所提方法与传统秩方法属于同一方法族，是其中的一个端点。

Conclusion: 基于代数的降维方法有效解决了传统秩方法在多组件弱相互作用动力系统中的线性掩蔽问题，在显著减少坐标数量的同时保证完整捕获系统组件，为复杂动力系统的降维预测提供了新途径。

Abstract: Dimension reduction for dynamical systems is standard practice, and the standard route is spectral: model the transfer (Koopman) operator by its leading modes. We show that on systems assembled from several weakly interacting components --- a structure common in physical and biological settings --- this may either require an exponential number of modes, or drop an entire component: the component is absent from the model rather than modeled coarsely, and no function of it can be predicted at any accuracy. We call this linear masking.
  The cause is that a rank-based model pays one coordinate per mode. We propose to score instead the $σ$-algebra the coordinates generate, so that products and powers come free and a component's cost is governed only by its generators rather than by all its interactions. The criterion is a $χ^2$-divergence between the embedded present and future, and it carries a budget guarantee: twice the intrinsic dimension of the dynamics is enough coordinates for an embedding whose algebra carries the operator's entire spectrum, with its full infinite rank.
  In variational form the criterion admits off-the-shelf estimators, and restricting its critic to the bilinear class returns the VAMP score on the span, so rank-based methods are one end of the same family. We demonstrate the proposed objective on a composite of published benchmark systems. We exhibit examples where the rank-based methods completely miss the masked components at all ranks $k<100$, while ten algebra coordinates recover all of them. In addition, the resulting algebra representation supports predicting the masked components from few labels, while direct regression from the high-dimensional observation or from the VAMP features fail.

</details>


### [70] [Lost in Aggregation: How Benchmarks Overlook Irreplaceable Model Strengths](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18919)
*Andrej Tschalzev,Stefan Lüdtke,Heiner Stuckenschmidt,Christian Bartelt*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 传统表格机器学习基准测试通过聚合指标评估模型，但这掩盖了模型在特定数据集上的不可替代性；研究提出数据为中心的峰值性能前沿框架，发现聚合指标主要衡量一致性而非独特优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有表格机器学习基准测试主要通过平均分数、排名或成对胜率等聚合指标来评估模型性能，这些聚合指标虽然有助于选择稳健的默认模型，但无法回答一个重要问题：哪些模型对于在特定数据集上达到峰值性能是必要的？作者认为基准评估应考虑数据为中心的峰值性能前沿。

Method: 提出数据为中心的峰值性能前沿框架，将模型分为四类：不可替代的、充分的、冗余的和易失效的，具体取决于它们在前沿上相对于其他模型的位置。该框架基于每个数据集上最佳统计支持性能来定义前沿边界。研究将此框架应用于TabArena基准测试进行分析。

Result: 在TabArena基准测试上的分析显示：1）常见聚合指标高度相关，主要衡量一致性和避免失败，而与数据集级别的不可替代性关联度较低；2）那些在各数据集上表现尚可但从未达到最佳选择的模型在聚合指标下被奖励；3）具有独特数据集特定优势的模型在聚合评估下表现平庸。

Conclusion: 基准测试进展的评估不应仅依赖于聚合指标的改进，还应考察新模型是否扩展了跨数据集可实现的峰值性能集合。这为模型评估提供了新的视角，强调了识别模型独特优势的重要性。

Abstract: Tabular machine learning benchmarks typically summarize performance by averaging scores, ranks, or pairwise wins across datasets. Such aggregates are useful for selecting robust default models, but they can obscure a different question: which models are necessary to attain peak performance on particular datasets? We argue that benchmark evaluation should also consider the data-centric peak performance frontier, defined by the best statistically supported performance achieved on each dataset. From this perspective, a model may be irreplaceable, sufficient, redundant, or fallible depending on where it lies on the frontier relative to other models. Applying this framework to the TabArena benchmark, we find that common aggregation metrics are highly correlated and largely measure consistency and avoiding failures, while being much less aligned with dataset-level irreplaceability. Consequently, models performing decently across datasets without ever being the best choice are rewarded while models with unique dataset-specific strengths appear mediocre under aggregation. Hence, benchmark progress should be measured not only by improvements on aggregation metrics but also by whether new models expand the set of attainable peak performances across datasets.

</details>


### [71] [Transportable Causal Effect Estimation across Networks under Interference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18932)
*Xiaojing Du,Jiuyong Li,Lin Liu,Debo Cheng,Jixue Liu,Thuc Duy Le*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了TranCE算法，解决网络干扰下跨网络迁移因果效应估计问题，通过选择图建模协变量偏移和网络结构偏移，推导出直接、溢出和总效应的迁移公式，并设计了双重稳健的估计方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有网络因果效应估计方法假设训练网络与部署网络相同，但实际应用中干预常在一个群体上实施，而研究问题却涉及另一个不同的群体，两者在拓扑结构、节点协变量组成和溢出路径上存在差异。这种跨网络迁移因果效应的数据融合问题尚无现有算法能够解决。

Method: 1. 将选择图扩展到网络设置，使协变量偏移和结构网络偏移作为独立的选择器进入模型；2. 基于选择图推导部署人群上直接、溢出和总效应的迁移公式，明确哪些干预机制被假设为不变，哪些观测分布需要重新加权；3. 提出TranCE（Transported Causal Effects）算法，结合干预结果模型、领域密度比校正和交叉拟合推断，实现双重稳健估计。

Result: 在两个源自真实社交网络的半合成基准和一个完全真实的天气保险田野实验上进行广泛实验，迁移效应与留出随机化估计进行对比验证，证明了方法的有效性。实验涵盖了协变量偏移和网络结构偏移两种场景。

Conclusion: 本研究为网络化系统（特别是社交网络和公共卫生领域）的干预策略优化提供了有效的跨网络因果效应迁移工具，解决了传统方法无法处理训练与部署网络不一致的实际问题。

Abstract: Estimating causal effects under network interference typically assumes that the network used for training and the network used for deployment coincide. In practice, an intervention is run on one population while the question of interest concerns a different population, and the two generally differ in topology, node-covariate composition, and spillover pathways. Transporting a causal effect across networks is therefore a data-fusion problem that no existing algorithm solves. We employ a selection diagram, extended to the network setting so that covariate shift and structural network shift enter as separate selectors, and derive from it a transport formula for the direct, spillover, and total effects in the deployment population. Each formula makes explicit which interventional mechanism is assumed invariant and which observational distribution must be reweighted. We then turn the formulas into TranCE (Transported Causal Effects), a doubly-robust algorithm combining an interventional outcome model, a domain density-ratio correction, and cross-fitted inference. Extensive experiments on two semi-synthetic benchmarks derived from real-world social networks and on a fully real weather-insurance field experiment, where the transported effects are checked against held-out randomized estimates, confirm the effectiveness of our approach. Our findings have the potential to improve intervention strategies in networked systems, particularly in social networks and public health.

</details>


### [72] [Graphical Design of Interpretable Architectures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18936)
*Pietro Barbiero*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于Penrose张量符号的图形化表示方法，用于设计和描述可解释AI架构。该方法能提供架构的全局视图，并与PyTorch einsum代码一一对应，解决了现有表示方法无法同时展示全局架构和具体张量操作的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
