Motivation: 现有的架构表示方法存在两个主要缺陷：符号方程无法提供架构的全局视图；概率图模型和流程图无法描述实际的张量操作，隐藏了关键见解并限制了可复现性。因此需要一个能够同时展示架构全局视图和具体张量操作的表示语言。

Method: 提出了一种改编自Penrose张量符号的图形化表示法。这种表示法能够提供架构的全局视图，并且与PyTorch einsum代码存在一一对应的映射关系。通过该符号描述了多种可解释架构，包括概念瓶颈、稀疏探针、原型网络、神经加性模型和线性模型混合等，并绘制了Steerling-8B语言模型的关键架构组件图。

Result: 成功将图形化表示法应用于多种可解释架构的描述。以Steerling-8B为例，该表示法能够揭示架构的全局洞察（如证明Steerling是一个残差模型），为每个独立操作提供几何解释，并能直接转化为33行PyTorch代码，验证了方法的实用性。

Conclusion: 所提出的图形化表示法有效填补了现有架构表示方法的空白，为可解释AI架构的设计、实现和比较提供了有力工具。该方法兼具全局视图展示和具体张量操作描述能力，显著提升了架构洞察力和代码可复现性。

Abstract: Designing, implementing, and comparing interpretable architectures requires a formal language to represent them. The most common representations fall short in one of two ways. Symbolic equations give no global view of an architecture at a glance. Probabilistic graphical models and flowcharts do not describe actual tensor manipulations, thus hiding key insights and limiting reproducibility. To close this gap, we introduce a graphical notation for designing interpretable AI architectures, adapted from Penrose tensor notation. This graphical notation gives a global view of an architecture and maps one to one onto PyTorch einsum code. We first use this notation to describe architectures that are interpretable by construction, including concept bottlenecks, sparse probes, prototype networks, neural additive models, and mixtures of linear models. We then diagram the key architectural components of Steerling-8B, a frontier interpretable language model. The diagram yields global insights into the architecture (e.g., showing that Steerling is a residual model), a geometric interpretation of each individual operation, and a direct translation into 33 lines of PyTorch code.

</details>


### [73] [Training Chemical Plausibility-Aware Large Language Models for Single-Step Retrosynthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18940)
*Bogdan Zagribelnyy,Ivan Ilin,Nikita Bondarev,Maksim Kuznetsov,Mathieu Reymond,Vladimir Aladinskiy,Alex Aliper,Alex Zhavoronkov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了Top-K提示方法和C3LM模型，利用超大规模数据集（4560万反应）训练，结合奖励机制在OOD基准上达到SOTA，发现LLM与传统模型探索的反应空间具有互补性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单步逆合成具有固有的“一对多”特性，但现有的单答案评估和基准协议无法有效捕捉这一特点，限制了模型的预测能力和评估准确性。

Method: 1. 提出Top-K提示作为训练和推理范式，以捕捉多样化的合理反应预测；2. 构建CREED-CCV-2+USPTO-XL超大规模数据集（约4560万已验证反应）；3. 基于该数据集训练C3LM（化学约束一致性语言模型）；4. 整合微调与ChemCensor奖励及新颖性奖励进行优化。

Result: 1. 在OOD URSA-expert-2026基准上达到最先进（SOTA）性能；2. 反应唯一性分析表明，LLM与传统模型探索互补的反应空间；3. 验证了基于集成的逆合成系统的潜力。

Conclusion: Top-K提示和合理性感知训练为未来基于LLM的合成规划提供了实用的稳健新方向，基于集成的逆合成系统具有进一步研究价值。

Abstract: Single-step retrosynthesis is a central component of computer-aided synthesis planning, yet its intrinsically one-to-many nature is poorly captured by single-answer evaluation and benchmarking protocols. To address this, we introduce Top-K prompting as a robust training and inference paradigm to better capture diverse, plausible reaction predictions. We compile CREED-CCV-2+USPTO-XL, an ultra-large-scale dataset of ~45.6 million verified reactions to train the C3LM (Chemistry Constraint-Consistent Language Model). By integrating fine-tuning with ChemCensor-based and novelty-oriented rewards, our model achieves state-of-the-art performance on the OOD URSA-expert-2026 benchmark. Further analysis of reaction uniqueness shows that LLMs and conventional models explore complementary reaction spaces, motivating ensemble-based retrosynthesis systems. Overall, our results establish Top-K, plausibility-aware training as a practical new direction for robust future LLM-based synthesis planning.

</details>


### [74] [Fuzzy Accuracy Compensates for Label Subjectivity in Classification of Skin Tone Using Wearable Photoplethysmography Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18969)
*Padmini Krishnadas,Urs Hackstein,Alen Bosnjakovic,Philip J. Aston*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了"模糊准确率"方法来评估PPG信号对Fitzpatrick肤色分类的性能，三种机器学习方法实现了42-55%的标准准确率，但模糊准确率高达85-96%，证明了PPG信号能够有效反映肤色差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 肤色分类任务的传统准确率较低（40-55%），但标签本身存在主观判断导致的小尺度误差（通过与比色卡对比确定），因此需要开发更合理的评估方法来准确衡量PPG信号对肤色分类的实际能力。

Method: 采用三种机器学习方法：(1)对原始PPG信号应用深度学习和树模型，引入模糊交叉熵损失函数；(2)基于SPAR方法生成信号图像表示后进行深度学习；(3)从信号中提取特征后进行机器学习。核心创新是提出"模糊准确率"评估指标，将预测类别与真实类别相差不超过一个类别视为正确。

Result: 树模型在原始信号上表现最佳：标准准确率55%，模糊准确率96%；深度学习在SPAR图像上达到44%准确率和85%模糊准确率；特征提取方法获得42%准确率和87%模糊准确率。

Conclusion: PPG信号受到肤色的可辨别影响，所提出的方法能够在最多一个类别误差范围内准确预测肤色等级，为肤色分类提供了更可靠的评估框架和建模方法。

Abstract: We consider the problem of classification of skin tone using photoplethysmography (PPG) signals with labels of the ordinal six-class Fitzpatrick skin tones. A typical accuracy for this task is a poor 40-55 %. However, the labels are subjectively determined by comparing the skin with a colour chart, and hence contain widespread small-scale inaccuracies. By working with a "fuzzy accuracy", which deems a prediction of skin tone class to be correct if its difference from the labelled class is not greater than one, much higher accuracy is obtained which provides more convincing evidence that skin tone can be accurately predicted from PPG signals. Three machine learning approaches were used, namely deep learning or tree-based approaches on raw PPG signals, deep learning on image representations of the signals generated by the Symmetric Projection Attractor Reconstruction (SPAR) method, and machine learning on features extracted from the signals. The first method also employed a fuzzy version of the cross entropy loss function, which gave the best results. Tree-based models on raw signals give accuracies up to 55 % and higher fuzzy accuracies up to 96 %, while deep learning models on the SPAR images obtained lower results of 44 % accuracy and 85 % fuzzy accuracy. The machine learning on PPG features gave similar results to the SPAR method with accuracy of 42 % and fuzzy accuracy of 87 %. We have shown that classification of skin tone using PPG signals is possible with high fuzzy accuracy which implies that our modelling approach enables accurate prediction of skin tone class within at most one class of the observer's choice of class, from which we conclude that PPG signals are affected by skin tone in a discernible way.

</details>


### [75] [Monroe: A Molecular Foundation Model for In-Context Probabilistic Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18982)
*Blazej Banaszewski,Andrew W. Fitzgibbon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了Monroe分子基础模型，通过扩大规模、改进立体化学表示、优化训练损失和多任务学习，结合TabPFN下游预测器，在分子活性和活性悬崖基准测试中达到或超越现有最先进方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生物活性预测面临数据受限问题，因为药物发现数据集依赖耗时且昂贵的湿实验进行数据生成和评估。这一挑战推动了分子基础模型研究，旨在将通用化学知识编码到分子表示中，以在数据受限场景下实现良好泛化。

Method: 1) 在PM6量子化学数据集的8100万分子上进行大规模预训练；2) 改进立体化学的图表示；3) 设计改进的训练损失（构象去噪和嵌入去相关）；4) 改进多任务学习；5) 使用先验数据拟合模型TabPFN进行下游上下文预测。采用有原则的成对比较框架评估统计显著的性能差异。

Result: 在Polaris基准测试中，Monroe匹配或超越现有分子基础模型；在评估分子发现实用性的活性悬崖基准测试中实现显著改进。消融和迁移实验表明，基于PFN的下游预测器显著改进了两个领先的现有模型MiniMol和CheMeleon，产生了新的最先进变体MiniMol_PFN和CheMeleon_PFN。

Conclusion: Monroe在分子基础模型领域取得显著进展，特别是在分子发现实用性方面。下游适应策略具有良好的泛化能力，不仅适用于Monroe，还能提升其他现有模型的性能，为药物发现中的数据受限问题提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Bioassay activity prediction is often data-limited because drug-discovery datasets rely on time-consuming and expensive wet-lab experiments for data generation and evaluation. This challenge has inspired recent research into molecular foundation models (MFMs), which aim to encode general-purpose chemical knowledge into molecular representations that generalize well in data-constrained scenarios. This paper presents Monroe, a new MFM with several innovations over the existing state of the art: increased scale allowing pre-training on over 81 million molecules from the PM6 quantum chemistry dataset; improved graph representation of stereochemistry; improved training losses including conformer denoising and embedding decorrelation; improved multi-task learning; and the use of a prior-data-fitted model (TabPFN) for downstream in-context prediction. Our evaluations use a principled pairwise comparison framework that measures statistically significant performance differences. Across established Polaris benchmarks, Monroe matches or exceeds existing MFMs, while on activity cliff benchmarks, designed to assess utility for molecular discovery, it achieves significant improvements over prior methods. Finally, ablation and transfer experiments show that PFN-based downstream predictors also substantially improve two leading existing models, MiniMol and CheMeleon, yielding new state-of-the-art variants we call MiniMol_PFN and CheMeleon_PFN, suggesting that our downstream adaptation strategy generalizes beyond Monroe. Source code is at github.com/blazejba/monroe.

</details>


### [76] [Harness Continual Learning: Continual Adaptation Beyond Model Parameters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19013)
*Borui Kang,Jinrui Gu,Junhan Lv,Wenbin Li,Lei Wang,Yang Gao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了Harness Continual Learning（HCL）新范式，通过在冻结的基础模型周围演化提示、记忆、工具等外部组件来实现持续学习，并设计了守护式更新机制有效缓解了马具级遗忘问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统持续学习主要关注模型参数的更新，但现代智能体通过提示、记忆、工具、技能和路由规则等外部组件组成的马具（Harness）来适应环境。这些外部内容的更新即使在不改变模型参数的情况下，也会破坏之前已掌握的可靠行为，这引发了新的挑战：如何在外部组件持续演进的同时保留之前获得的能力？

Method: 1. 形式化定义了Harness Continual Learning（HCL）范式，将马具级遗忘定义为早期行为的丢失；2. 设计了四个面向执行的组件：任务接口、经验记忆、能力图谱和自适应路由器；3. 提出了守护式马具演化机制，包含持续优化器（根据执行后反馈生成候选马具）和持续评估器（在验证当前改进、历史保留和有效性后才提交更新）。

Result: 在文本推理、多模态感知和开放世界交互三个任务上的实验表明：1. 实现了能力累积和故障恢复；2. 在多个设置下相对基线提升超过10%；3. 消融实验验证了各马具组件的贡献；4. 受控保留实验揭示了可测量的马具级遗忘，并表明稳定性-可塑性权衡可以显式调节。

Conclusion: HCL范式成功将持续学习从模型中心扩展到马具层面，通过守护式更新机制在冻结基础模型的前提下实现了外部组件的持续演进与能力保持的平衡，为现代智能体的持续学习提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Continual learning has largely been model-centric, treating model parameters as the state that changes with sequential experience. Modern agents can also adapt through a harness of prompts, memories, tools, skills, and routing rules. Because these contents jointly shape later execution, a harness update can disrupt previously reliable behavior even when the model is frozen. This raises a new question: how can an agent continually improve its state outside the model while retaining behavior acquired earlier? We formulate Harness Continual Learning (HCL), a new continual learning paradigm in which the harness evolves around a frozen foundation model, and define the resulting loss of earlier behavior as harness-level forgetting. We instantiate HCL with four execution-facing components: the Task Interface, Experience Memory, Capability Map, and Adaptive Router. We further introduce guarded harness evolution to separate update generation from state commitment. A Continual Optimizer proposes candidate harnesses from post-execution feedback, and a Continual Evaluator commits the resulting candidate harness only after checking current improvement, historical retention, and validity. Experiments on textual reasoning, multimodal perception, and open-world interaction demonstrate capability accumulation and failure recovery, with relative gains exceeding 10% over corresponding baselines in multiple settings. Component ablations assess the contribution of each harness component, while controlled retention sweeps reveal measurable harness-level forgetting and show that the stability--plasticity trade-off can be explicitly adjusted.

</details>


### [77] [Multi-Agent Off-Policy Deep Reinforcement Learning for Smart Campus Coverage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19049)
*Omar Rady,Mohamed Ayman,Ali Arafa,Mohamed Shalma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了在非凸校园拓扑中毫米波基站的最优部署问题，通过对比四种深度强化学习方案，发现多智能体DDPG方法在密集场景下表现最佳，实现了全覆盖和0.94的Jain公平性指数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 毫米波基站在现实非凸拓扑中的最优部署是一个NP难问题，由于最大-最小公平性目标的非凸、非光滑特性，传统优化方法难以有效求解。深度强化学习因其实时适应性和在复杂优化问题中的有效性，为解决这一挑战提供了新的途径。

Method: 将基站部署问题建模为马尔可夫决策过程(MDP)，系统性地对比评估四种深度强化学习方案：离散单智能体DQN、空间分区多智能体DQN、连续单智能体DDPG以及地理分区多智能体DDPG框架。

Result: 多智能体DDPG方法在密集场景中显著优于单智能体方法。实现了全覆盖，并获得0.94的Jain公平性指数。在400用户的密集场景中，多智能体方法展现出高效的计算收敛性能。

Conclusion: 多智能体深度确定性策略梯度(DDPG)框架是解决毫米波基站部署优化问题的有效方法，特别适用于密集用户场景，能够在保证公平性的同时实现高效计算收敛和全覆盖。

Abstract: Deep reinforcement learning (DRL) has recently gained a great attention due to its real-time adaptation and effectiveness in complex optimization problems. This paper investigates the optimal deployment of millimeter-wave (mmWave) base stations (BSs) in a realistic, non-convex campus topology. The optimization problem is NP-hard, due to the non-convex, non-smooth nature of the max-min fairness objective. To overcome these constraints, we formulate the BS placement as a Markov Decision Process (MDP) and systematically benchmark four DRL schemes: a discrete single-agent Deep Q-Network (DQN), a spatially partitioned Multi-Agent DQN, a continuous single-agent Deep Deterministic Policy Gradient (DDPG), and a geographically partitioned multi-agent DDPG framework. Numerical evaluations reveal that the multi-agent DDPG approach substantially outperforms single-agent in dense scenarios. Additionally full coverage is achieved, and a fairness Jain's index of 0.94 is obtained. Finally, the multi-agent demonstrates highly efficient computational convergence of dense scenarios with $400$ users.

</details>


### [78] [Does Mapping Non-Maximal Probabilities to GMM Components Matter for S-JEPA Encoder Representations?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19084)
*Wenxuan He,Yunpeng Li,Shan Liang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: S-JEPA中软高斯混合模型(GMM)后验概率的数值结构本身不能完全决定编码器学习到的表示，非最大概率值分配到哪些GMM组件同样重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: S-JEPA使用软GMM后验概率而非硬聚类标签来保留不确定性，但目前尚不清楚仅概率数值是否足够，还是非最大概率值分配到哪些GMM组件也会产生影响。

Method: 设计了两个匹配控制实验：FIXED-RANDPERM保留top-1组件和概率以及非最大概率值的多重集，但使用每个物理帧固定的映射重新分配这些非最大值；UNIFORM-TAIL保留top-1组件、其概率和总非最大质量，但将该质量均匀分布。在三个独立随机种子上进行实验对比。

Result: 在两个冻结编码器读出任务上，真实软目标(REAL SOFT)优于两个控制条件。它提供了更好的原始GMM尾部恢复，并在控制当前帧完整频谱后，在短时间尺度上提供更好的频谱动态可访问性。在两个暴露实验中，随着更多帧保留原始映射，两个读出任务整体都有改善。

Conclusion: 软目标的数值概率结构不能完全决定学习到的编码器表示。非最大概率到GMM组件的映射也很重要，这为理解软标签在学习中的作用提供了新的见解。

Abstract: S-JEPA uses soft Gaussian mixture model (GMM) posteriors instead of hard cluster labels to preserve uncertainty. It remains unclear whether the probability values alone are sufficient, or whether it also matters which GMM components receive the non-maximal probabilities. We test this with two matched controls. FIXED-RANDPERM keeps the top-1 component and probability together with the multiset of non-maximal probability values, but reassigns those non-maximal values using a mapping fixed for each physical frame. UNIFORM-TAIL keeps the top-1 component, its probability, and total non-maximal mass but distributes that mass uniformly. Across three independent seeds, REAL SOFT outperforms both controls on two frozen Encoder readouts. It provides better recovery of the original GMM tail and greater accessibility of spectral dynamics over short time scales after controlling for the complete spectrum of the current frame. In two exposure experiments, both readouts improved overall as more frames retained the original mapping. We also descriptively follow one Phase 2 trajectory after the switch to the online GMM. These results show that the numerical probability structure of the soft target does not fully determine the learned Encoder representation. The mapping of non-maximal probabilities to GMM components also matters.

</details>


### [79] [Open-MOPD: Diagnosing and Fixing Capability Imbalance in Multi-Teacher On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19098)
*Huan-ang Gao,Haohan Chi,Yong Yan,Shiyuan Feng,Hanlin Wu,Zheng Jiang,Bingxiang He,Wei-Ying Ma,Ya-Qin Zhang,Hao Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了多教师在线策略蒸馏（M-OPD）中存在的能力整合差距问题，提出Open-MOPD框架通过三种机制将头部空间恢复率从35.6%提升至83.4%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多教师在线策略蒸馏虽然在实际应用中取得成功，但其优化动力学机制尚未被充分理解，且缺乏可复现的开放方案。作者旨在系统研究多教师能力整合过程中存在的问题并提出解决方案。

Method: 在SmolLM3-3B-Base上建立受控的M-OPD基准测试，采用oracle路由消除路由歧义。针对发现的三种正交因素（结构性序列长度差异、动态收敛漂移、多步奖励陈旧性），提出Open-MOPD框架，包含token共享平衡、间隙感知的动态预算分配和学生奖励刷新三种机制。

Result: 标准M-OPD仅捕获35.6%的可用头部空间，简洁任务（如指令跟随）出现严重退化。Open-MOPD框架系统性地恢复跨域平衡，将头部空间恢复率从35.6%提升至83.4%，并在学术可访问的硬件预算下开源了完整的后训练方案、训练轨迹和评估套件。

Conclusion: 多教师能力整合失败的根本原因是token级优化预算的严重错误分配，而非梯度冲突。通过Open-MOPD的三种机制可以有效解决结构性序列长度差异、动态收敛漂移和多步奖励陈旧性导致的优化预算失衡问题。

Abstract: Multi-teacher on-policy distillation (M-OPD) has emerged as a promising paradigm for consolidating domain-specialized reinforcement learning (RL) experts into a single generalist student via dense, token-level reward supervision. Despite its practical success, the optimization dynamics governing multi-teacher capability integration remain poorly understood, and open, rigorously reproducible recipes are conspicuously lacking. In this work, we establish a controlled M-OPD benchmark on SmolLM3-3B-Base with oracle routing, isolating capability integration from routing ambiguity. Our investigation reveals a pronounced capability integration gap: standard M-OPD captures only 35.6% of the available headroom relative to a domain-routed oracle ensemble, with concise tasks such as instruction following suffering severe degradation and premature stagnation. Crucially, we show that this failure stems not from gradient conflict, but from a severe misallocation of the token-level optimization budget. This pathology is driven by three orthogonal factors: structural sequence-length disparities across domains, dynamic convergence drift due to non-uniform learning rates, and multi-step reward staleness from asynchronous policy updates. To resolve these imbalances, we introduce Open-MOPD, a principled framework incorporating token-share balancing, gap-aware dynamic budget allocation, and student reward refresh. Together, these mechanisms systematically restore cross-domain balance, elevating headroom recovery from 35.6% to 83.4% in a single deployable student. We fully open-source our end-to-end post-training recipe, training trajectories, and evaluation suites on an academically accessible hardware budget.

</details>


### [80] [Pretraining Reusable Inference Across Views with Synthetic Task Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19115)
*Jielong Lu,Zhihao Wu,Jiajun Yu,Zhaoliang Chen,Haishuai Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出SIMPLE，一种先验拟合的多视图上下文学习器，通过预训练推理过程而非融合函数，实现了多视图推理的可复用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代预训练编码器使多视图表示可复用，但现有的多视图学习方法仍需为每个下游任务重新学习视图效用判断和证据组合过程，导致关于视图相关性、互补性、可靠性和缺失性的知识被反复丢弃而非跨任务迁移。

Method: 1. 将多视图学习重构为学习可复用的任务条件推理过程；2. 构建可控的合成任务先验，生成多样化的支持-查询片段，涵盖不同的类别结构、共享/视图特定因子、表示几何、跨视图依赖、可靠性水平、缺失模式和分布偏移；3. 设计分层推理架构，分别在视图内、跨视图、跨支持-查询样本三个层级进行推理。

Result: 在多视图和多组学基准测试中，SIMPLE的冻结版本无需更新推理主干即可达到竞争性能；轻量级适配器校准在大多数评估数据集上取得领先性能。在冻结、单样本、缺失视图等多种设置下均表现出色。

Conclusion: 多视图推理过程本身可以被预训练和复用，验证了核心假设；轻量级适配器校准可在需要时提供任务特定的对齐，实现了推理过程可迁移性与任务适应性的有效平衡。

Abstract: Modern pretrained encoders make representations from heterogeneous views increasingly reusable, but the procedure that determines view utility and combines evidence is still relearned for each downstream task. Consequently, knowledge about view relevance, complementarity, reliability, and missingness is repeatedly discarded rather than transferred across tasks. We therefore reformulate multi-view learning as learning a reusable, task-conditioned inference procedure rather than a fixed fusion function. Based on this perspective, we propose SIMPLE, a prior-fitted multi-view in-context learner that predicts query labels by conditioning on a small labeled support set. Since existing real-world datasets cover only a limited range of view configurations and task structures, we construct a controllable synthetic task prior in embedding space. It generates diverse support-query episodes with varying class structures, shared and view-specific factors, representation geometries, cross-view dependencies, reliability levels, missingness patterns, and distribution shifts. A hierarchical inference architecture then performs reasoning within views, across views, and across support and query samples. Experiments on multi-view and multi-omics benchmarks demonstrate that the frozen variant of SIMPLE achieves competitive performance without updating the inference backbone, while lightweight adapter calibration attains leading performance on most evaluated datasets. Together, the results under frozen, one-shot, and missing-view settings support the central hypothesis that multi-view reasoning itself can be pretrained and reused, while lightweight adapter calibration provides task-specific alignment when needed.

</details>


### [81] [Enhancing EBSD throughput of battery electrode materials using super-resolution generative adversarial networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19117)
*John Mangum,Andrew Glaws,Francois Usseglio-Viretta,Steven Spurgeon,Donal Finegan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究开发了一种基于生成对抗网络(SRGAN)的超分辨率框架，可将EBSD采集速度提高25倍或视野扩大25倍，同时保持可接受的微观结构分析精度，为锂离子电池电极材料表征提供了一种高通量解决方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电子背散射衍射(EBSD)是锂离子电池电极材料微观结构定量表征的关键方法，但其固有的慢速特性阻碍了获得统计代表性微结构数据集所需的分析通量，限制了其在材料研发和工业过程开发中的应用效率。

Method: 采用生成对抗网络(SRGAN)构建超分辨率框架，在LiNixMnyCozO2 (NMC)正极颗粒的EBSD数据上进行训练，通过计算增强低分辨率数据集。将SRGAN方法与经典插值方法在2x到12x的不同放大倍数下进行系统比较，并使用定性图像指标和定量微观结构分析进行评估。

Result: SRGAN在保留小晶粒和维持真实晶界方面系统性地优于经典方法。在5x放大倍数下(对应25倍采集速度提升或25倍视野扩大)，晶粒等效面积直径、晶粒最大内切球直径和晶界长度的相对误差分别为+5.7%、+8.2%和-14.6%，验证了该方法在保持关键指标可接受精度下的实用性。

Conclusion: SRGAN方法显著提高了EBSD采集效率，实现了更统计稳健的微观结构数据集获取，使EBSD成为材料研究和工业过程开发的高通量表征工具，为锂离子电池电极材料优化提供了有力支持。

Abstract: Quantitative microstructural characterization of Li-ion battery electrode materials using electron backscatter diffraction (EBSD) has been proven as a critical method for optimizing cell performance. However, the inherently slow nature of EBSD can hinder the throughput of analyses needed for statistical representation of a material microstructure being developed. This work demonstrates a machine learning super-resolution framework using a generative adversarial network (SRGAN) to significantly increase EBSD throughput. The SRGAN model was trained on EBSD data of LiNixMnyCozO2 (NMC) cathode particles to computationally enhance low-resolution datasets and its performance is compared against classical interpolation methods across various upscaling factors (2x to 12x). Both qualitative image metrics and quantitative microstructural analysis verified that the SRGAN systematically outperformed classical methods, particularly in preserving small grains and maintaining realistic grain boundaries. We demonstrate that a 5x upscaling factor, corresponding to a 25x speed-up in acquisition time or a 25x larger field of view, is practical while maintaining acceptable accuracy in key metrics like grain size and shape. For instance, at 5x upscaling, relative errors were +5.7%, +8.2%, and -14.6% on grain area-equivalent diameter, grain maximum sphere-inscribed diameter, and grain boundary length, respectively. The SRGAN methodology developed in this work significantly enhances the efficiency of EBSD acquisition for more statistically robust microstructural dataset, enabling EBSD as a high-throughput characterization tool for materials research and industrial process development.

</details>


### [82] [Discretizing Continuous Time Series for Imputation with Masked Diffusion Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19119)
*Dongbin Kim,Seungyun Lee,Geonwoo Shin,Jaewook Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了MDTIM模型，通过引入与有效观测正交的MASK token和随机离散化技术，解决了现有时间序列填充方法中表征空间未分离和学习目标错位的问题，实现了更优的鲁棒性和可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时间序列填充方法存在两个关键局限性：1) 缺失值和观测值在同一表征空间中编码，缺乏显式的结构分离；2) 连续扩散模型训练目标是预测添加的噪声而非原始信号，与填充任务目标不一致。这促使作者设计一个更契合填充任务本质的模型架构。

Method: 提出Masked Diffusion Time-series Imputation Model (MDTIM)：1) 利用掩码扩散模型训练范式，设计结构上与有效观测正交的MASK token，实现表征空间分离；2) 模型直接预测原始值而非噪声，与填充任务目标自然对齐；3) 引入Stochastic Discretization技术，将连续值映射为有序感知的token，弥合离散掩码扩散与时间序列连续有序特性之间的鸿沟，同时保留连续动态特性。

Result: 在多个多样化基准数据集上的实验表明：MDTIM在各种缺失场景下始终优于最先进的确定性方法和生成式基线；模型展现出更优越的鲁棒性和可扩展性；验证了结构正交设计和目标对齐策略的有效性。

Conclusion: MDTIM通过表征空间的结构化分离和学习目标的重新对齐，有效解决了时间序列填充中的核心挑战。掩码扩散范式结合随机离散化技术，为处理时间序列的复杂时序动态和噪声问题提供了新的解决思路，在实际应用中具有广阔前景。

Abstract: Time series imputation is a crucial area for reliable time series analysis, yet it remains challenging due to the complex temporal dynamics and noise of real-world data. Existing approaches, however, exhibit two limitations: missing and observed values are embedded within the same representation space without explicit structural separation, and continuous diffusion-based methods are trained to predict added noise rather than the original signal. To address these, we propose the Masked Diffusion Time-series Imputation Model (MDTIM), which leverages the training paradigm of masked diffusion model for imputation tasks. The MASK token is structurally orthogonal to valid observations, and the model directly predicts the original values, naturally aligning both the representation and the learning objective with the imputation task. To bridge the gap between discrete masked diffusion and the continuous, ordinal nature of time series, we further introduce Stochastic Discretization, which maps continuous values to ordinal-aware tokens while preserving continuous dynamics. Our experiments on diverse benchmarks confirm that MDTIM achieves superior robustness and scalability, consistently outperforming state-of-the-art deterministic and generative baselines across various missing scenarios.

</details>


### [83] [PGFS++: Molecular Property Improvement under Synthesis and Diversity Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19121)
*Boqiao Zhang,Godbless James,Sai Krishna Gottipati,Andrew Fitzgibbon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了PGFS++，一种合成感知的强化学习框架，用于分子性质优化。相比前代PGFS+存在的"奖励黑客"问题（将不同输入分子映射到同一高分分子导致输出多样性降低），PGFS++通过输入特定的正向合成轨迹规划，在提升目标性质的同时保持了结构相似性和输出多样性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 早期药物发现中分子性质优化（如类药性、结合亲和力）是核心任务，但无约束化学空间中的优化分子若无法合成则缺乏实用价值。现有的合成感知强化学习方法PGFS存在反应物嵌入预测导致选择不直接、学习效率受限的问题，而改进后的PGFS+虽然提升了目标性质，却出现了奖励黑客现象——强大的反应物搜索会将不同输入分子映射到同一个高分"磁铁分子"，在提高奖励的同时导致输出多样性崩溃。

Method: 首先开发PGFS+，使用可训练的嵌入查找表表示反应模板和第二反应物，结合更有效的评分函数和强化学习算法。在此基础上提出PGFS++框架：将输入分子作为正向合成轨迹的起点，应用学习到的反应模板与兼容的库存构建块，生成具有改进目标性质、明确合成路线且与输入保持结构相似性的分子。该方法实现了输入特定的分子改进，而非将所有输入收敛到同一输出。

Result: PGFS+相比原版PGFS显著提升了目标性质，但暴露了奖励黑客失效模式——多样化输入分子被映射到相同的高奖励"磁铁分子"，导致输出多样性崩溃。PGFS++在分子改进任务实验中证明了其有效性：在提升目标性质的同时保持了高输出多样性，并提供了明确的合成路线和与输入分子的结构相似性。

Conclusion: PGFS++成功解决了合成感知分子优化中奖励与多样性的权衡问题。通过输入特定的正向合成规划，该方法避免了将不同输入收敛到同一高分子的陷阱，为早期药物发现提供了一个既具有合成可行性又能保持化学空间多样性的实用优化工具，在保证分子可合成性的同时实现了有效的性质改进。

Abstract: Improving molecular properties, such as drug-likeness or binding affinity, is a recurring task in early-stage drug discovery. However, molecules optimized in an unconstrained chemical space have limited practical value if they cannot be synthesized. Policy Gradient for Forward Synthesis (PGFS) is a synthesis-aware reinforcement learning method for molecular improvement, but its use of reactant embedding prediction makes reactant selection indirect, which, as we show, limits learning effectiveness. We first develop PGFS+, in which reaction templates and second reactants are represented by trainable embedding lookup tables. Combined with a more effective scoring function and RL algorithm, PGFS+ significantly improves the desired property. However, it exposes a reward-hacking failure mode: a powerful reactant search can map diverse input molecules to the same high-reward magnet molecule, improving the reward while collapsing the output diversity. We therefore introduce PGFS++, a synthesis-aware reinforcement learning framework for input-specific molecular improvement. Given an input molecule, PGFS++ treats it as the start of a forward-synthesis trajectory, applies learned reaction templates with compatible in-stock building blocks, and produces a molecule with improved target properties, an explicit synthesis route, and structural similarity to the input. Experiments on molecular improvement tasks show that PGFS++ improves target properties while preserving high output diversity.

</details>


### [84] [Leaf Values as Coordinates: Exact Contrastive Explanation for Gradient-Boosted Ensembles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19127)
*Emanuele Luzio*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将梯度提升树的叶节点值视为坐标，将实例映射到线性空间，从而实现精确的对比解释和反事实建议方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统方法需要拟合、采样或假设特征加性，而梯度提升树本身在适当空间中已具有加性结构。通过视角转换，可以精确地解释模型决策差异，并为被拒绝申请者提供可行的修改建议。

Method: 将每棵树的叶节点值视为坐标，把实例映射到R^M空间，在此空间中模型线性作用。差异向量在共享叶节点处为零，仅少数坐标承载实例间差异，每个坐标可追溯到真实树的分裂。在此基础上构建反事实建议方法，限制建议为被解释对象实际可执行的变更。

Result: 在五个表格数据集上通过重复交叉验证评估：建议重构模型决策精度达6.2×10^-15；在信用数据集上Pareto非劣于努力与真实性权衡；当限制为可行变更时，有效性保留58%（最强基线仅41%）。

Conclusion: 该方法无需拟合、采样或假设加性，实现了精确且可追溯的对比解释；考虑可行性约束后显著优于基线，揭示了标准评估忽略了建议可执行性的问题。

Abstract: A gradient-boosted ensemble predicts by summing one leaf value per tree. Read
  those values as coordinates rather than as intermediate results, and every
  instance becomes a point in R^M on which the model acts linearly: the score is
  the sum of the coordinates.
  This small change of view makes contrastive explanation exact. The difference
  between two instances is a vector that is identically zero wherever they share
  a leaf, so the gap between a rejected applicant and an accepted one is carried
  by a handful of coordinates, each traceable to a real split in a real tree.
  Nothing is fitted, sampled, or assumed additive in features -- the additivity
  is already there, in the right space.
  We build a recourse method on this representation and evaluate it on five
  tabular datasets under repeated cross-validation. Its recommendation
  reconstructs the model's own decision to 6.2 x 10^-15, so an auditor can
  re-check the arithmetic without the model. On the credit datasets it is
  Pareto-non-dominated on effort against realism. And when recommendations are
  restricted to changes the subject could actually make -- not their age, not a
  settled delinquency -- it retains 58% of its validity where the strongest
  baseline retains 41%, a distinction the standard evaluation cannot see because
  it never asks whether a recommendation can be carried out.

</details>


### [85] [Beyond Trial Averaging: Anchoring Neural and Visual Representations for Few-Repetition Brain-to-Image Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19128)
*Zhenyao Cui,Siyuan Kan,Dingkun Liu,Dongrui Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出NEAR框架，通过锚点对齐方法解决少重复神经信号下的图像检索问题，显著提升检索精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有脑信号图像检索方法需要大量重复试验(高达80次)才能达到良好性能，但在仅有1-4次重复时准确率急剧下降。传统观点将其归因于查询噪声，但本文发现低重复查询信号和图像表示之间存在非传递性对齐模式，揭示库位置也是影响检索的关键因素。

Method: 提出基于神经锚点的检索框架(NEAR)，将高重复中心视为锚点：1)去噪器将噪声查询拉向真实锚点；2)小型网络从图像预测候选的伪锚点并将图像表示拉向锚点，实现双向对齐。

Result: 在跨EEG、MEG和fMRI四个数据集上持续改善少重复场景检索性能。在THINGS-EEG2数据集上，200路Top-1准确率在1次和4次重复时分别提升5.7和9.3个百分点。

Conclusion: NEAR通过锚定神经和视觉表示，降低对重复采集的依赖，使神经检索更接近实际部署应用。

Abstract: Decoding visual information from brain signals probes neural representations and enables neuro-rehabilitation and dream decoding. Recent brain-to-image retrieval approaches have achieved promising performance, typically by averaging many (up to 80) neural trials per image, requiring repeated stimulus presentation that increases latency, cost, and user burden. When only one or a few repetitions are available, the retrieval accuracy drops sharply. This drop is commonly attributed to query noise because averaging suppresses noise and increases signal stability. However, we find a non-transitive alignment pattern: the low-repetition query signal and the image representation each align with the high-repetition center, but not directly with each other. This pattern shows that query noise is only part of the problem and that gallery placement also affects retrieval. We therefore propose a neural-anchor-based retrieval (NEAR) framework that treats the high-repetition center as an anchor and approaches it from both sides: a denoiser pulls the noisy query toward the true anchor, and a small network predicts each candidate's pseudo anchor from its image and pulls the image toward it. Across four datasets spanning EEG, MEG and fMRI, NEAR consistently improved retrieval in the few-repetition regime. On THINGS-EEG2, it improved 200-way Top-1 accuracy by 5.7 and 9.3 percentage points respectively, when averaging one and four repetitions. By anchoring neural and visual representations, NEAR reduces reliance on repeated acquisition and brings neural retrieval closer to real-world deployment.

</details>


### [86] [SCORE: Subject Coordinate Recovery for Label-Free Cross-Subject EEG-to-Image Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19134)
*Zhenyao Cui,Siyuan Kan,Siyang Li,Ziwei Wang,Dongrui Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出SCORE框架，通过坐标系对齐解决EEG跨被试视觉解码问题，在无目标标签情况下显著提升新用户的图像检索性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前EEG到图像检索方法在新用户无标注校准时性能远低于被试内方法，限制了实际应用。分析发现不同被试保留了相似的概念关系，但表达方式沿不同坐标方向，这为跨被试迁移提供了理论基础。

Method: 提出SCORE框架，包含：(1)恢复感知的源域训练，将源被试EEG与公共图像空间对齐，通过仅源域情节模拟未见被试恢复；(2)部署时的坐标对齐，冻结编码器后通过中心性校正匹配选择可靠的EEG-图像地标点，估计正交变换恢复目标EEG坐标，无需源数据或目标标签。

Result: 在THINGS-EEG2和Alljoined-1.6M数据集上，200路检索任务中SCORE对所有目标被试均优于未适配基线，达到53.23%/83.55%和12.01%/32.16%的Top-1/Top-5准确率，分别超越最强基线17.45/15.70和3.08/4.62个百分点。

Conclusion: SCORE框架在无需目标标签和编码器更新的情况下，有效缩小了跨被试与被试内性能差距，使基于大脑的视觉解码更接近跨用户的鲁棒、实用、低延迟部署。

Abstract: Accurate visual decoding can reveal how the brain represents visual information and recover perceived content from neural signals such as electroencephalography (EEG), with potential for neural communication. However, current EEG-to-image retrieval methods perform far below their within-subject counterparts for new users without labeled calibration, limiting real-world deployment. To understand this gap, we analyze EEG features across subjects and find that different subjects preserve similar relationships among concepts but express them along different coordinate directions. We therefore propose Subject Coordinate Recovery (SCORE), a target label-free framework combining recovery-aware source training with coordinate alignment at deployment. During training, SCORE aligns source subject EEG with a common image space and simulates unseen-subject recovery through source-only episodes. At deployment, with both encoders frozen, SCORE selects reliable EEG-image landmarks through hubness-corrected matching and estimates an orthogonal transformation to recover target EEG coordinates without source data or target labels. In 200-way retrieval on two public benchmarks, SCORE outperforms the unadapted baseline for every target subject and achieves the best overall accuracy. It reaches 53.23%/83.55% and 12.01%/32.16% Top-1/Top-5 on THINGS-EEG2 and Alljoined-1.6M, respectively, surpassing the strongest baselines by 17.45/15.70 and 3.08/4.62 percentage points. Without target labels or encoder updates, SCORE brings brain-based visual decoding closer to robust, practical, low-latency deployment across users.

</details>


### [87] [Bridge Graphical Models: Coupling, Projection, and Current-Preserving Dynamics for Generative Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19144)
*Tiantian Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文引入了“Markovization gap”这一新概念，用于度量端点条件桥（endpoint-conditioned bridges）压缩为非预测性马尔可夫解码器时不可避免的信息损失，并提出了Bridge Graphical Models (BGMs)框架统一描述多种生成模型。实验表明，该gap可在训练前快速预测模型性能，为桥和耦合设计提供了预训练诊断工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续时间生成模型（如扩散模型、流匹配、整流流、薛定谔桥等）通常基于端点条件桥构建，但实际生成过程需要一个非预测性马尔可夫解码器（仅观察当前状态和时间）。作者识别出这种桥到解码器的压缩是一个结构瓶颈，会导致不可约简的信息损失，但目前缺乏量化方法来评估和比较不同模型设计的影响。

Method: 1. 提出Markovization gap：定义为桥速度在给定马尔可夫状态下的时间积分条件方差，即在采样器可用信息下预测端点条件运动的最小均方误差(MMSE)。

2. 构建Bridge Graphical Models (BGMs)框架：将端点耦合、桥定律、马尔可夫投影和电流保持动力学表示分离为独立的设计选择，使不同模型族之间的瓶颈可比较。

3. 将泊松和静电模型统一表示为具有相应场线马尔可夫化gap的场线桥核。

4. 开发特征空间代理gap：在训练前快速估计设计选择的质量。

Result: 在CIFAR-10和Fashion-MNIST上的合成、潜空间和像素空间实验中，特征空间代理gap（在训练前几分钟内估计）与下游训练损失和FID分数呈一致的排序关系。这表明Markovization gap能够正确预测不同设计选择的相对性能，且该方法在固定架构、桥、采样器和计算预算下有效。

Conclusion: Markovization gap作为一种预训练诊断指标，成功量化了桥到解码器压缩过程中不可避免的损失。BGMs框架统一了多种生成模型的表示，使得设计选择可比较且可优化。该工作为生成模型的桥和耦合设计提供了理论指导和实践工具，有望加速模型架构搜索和性能预测。

Abstract: Continuous-time generative models are often built from endpoint-conditioned bridges, but generation requires a different object: a non-anticipative Markov decoder that only observes the current state and time. We identify this bridge-to-decoder compression as a structural bottleneck shared by diffusion models, flow matching, rectified flow, Schrödinger bridges, and field-based generative models. We introduce the \emph{Markovization gap}, the time-integrated conditional variance of the bridge velocity given the Markov state. It is the MMSE of predicting endpoint-conditioned motion from the information available to a sampler, and it measures an irreducible loss incurred before any neural network is trained. To make this bottleneck comparable across model families, we define \emph{Bridge Graphical Models} (BGMs), which separate endpoint coupling, bridge law, Markovian projection, and current-preserving dynamics representation as independent design choices. The same formalism also represents Poisson and electrostatic models as field-line bridge kernels with a corresponding field-line Markovization gap. Across synthetic, latent, and pixel-space pilots on CIFAR-10 and Fashion-MNIST, a feature-space proxy gap estimated in minutes before training ranks design choices in the same direction as downstream training loss and FID under fixed architecture, bridge, sampler, and compute. These results support the Markovization gap as a pre-training diagnostic for bridge and coupling design.

</details>


### [88] [Continuous-Time Reinforcement Learning for Controlled Hawkes Jump-Diffusions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19151)
*Tomasz R. Bielecki,Thibaut Mastrolia,Haoze Yan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了非马尔可夫环境下多变量霍克斯驱动的随机微分方程的随机控制问题，提出了一种马尔可夫化近似方法和连续时间强化学习算法来解决这一难题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于霍克斯过程的强度具有路径依赖性和记忆性，该问题无法直接应用经典的马尔可夫随机控制理论（除特定核函数外），因此需要开发新的理论框架和算法来处理这种非马尔可夫特性。

Method: 首先开发了有限维马尔可夫化程序，用指数核混合近似多变量霍克斯过程，并证明了近似过程和问题价值的收敛性；然后提出了基于马尔可夫化近似的连续时间确定性策略梯度学习方法（Hawkes-CT DDPG），这是一种无模型算法，仅需观测事件时间、SDE解的实现和选定的衰减滤波器，而无需已知霍克斯核系数。

Result: 在三种不同类型核函数（简单指数核、Erlang核和幂律核）下，将所提出的连续时间强化学习方法与离散时间强化学习技术进行了性能比较。

Conclusion: 成功建立了非马尔可夫霍克斯驱动优化问题的理论和算法框架，通过马尔可夫化近似和连续时间强化学习实现了对原始问题的有效求解，为解决路径依赖的随机控制问题提供了新思路。

Abstract: We study stochastic control of multivariate Hawkes-driven stochastic differential equations with machine learning algorithms in a non-Markovian setting. Due to the path dependence of the memory of the Hawkes intensity, this problem does not fall within classical stochastic control theory outside particular Markovian kernels. We first develop a finite-dimensional Markovianization procedure and algorithm to approximate multivariate Hawkes processes with mixtures of exponential kernels. We prove the convergence of the Markovianized approximation of the Hawkes process, its intensity, and the value of the problem to the original non-Markovian processes and the value of the primal problem. We then formulate continuous-time deterministic policy gradient learning on the Markovianized approximation of the problem, called Hawkes-CT DDPG. We propose a model-free algorithm to solve the non-Markovian Hawkes-driven optimization by observing only the event times of the process, the realization of the solution to the SDE, and a chosen set of decay filters, while the Hawkes kernel coefficients remain unknown. We compare our continuous time reinforcement learning Hawkes-CT DDPG method with discrete time reinforcement learning techniques under three different types of kernels: simple exponential, Erlang, and power-law kernels.

</details>


### [89] [Learned, Then Lost: A Measured Single-Example Counterfactual in Pre-training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19168)
*Zachary Speck,Asa Shepard*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 通过24次反事实实验，首次直接测量单个训练样本对GPT-2模型的影响，发现样本注入会在训练早期产生短暂影响，随后衰减，最终仅使模型在参数盆地内发生位移而不改变盆地位置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法仅能估计单个训练样本对最终模型的贡献，因为精确测量需要运行两次仅相差一个样本的完整预训练，计算成本极高。研究者希望通过实际测量而非估计来理解单个样本对模型的因果影响。

Method: 从零训练32个124M参数的GPT-2模型，设置四种条件（三种注入条件：语料库已验证主题的流畅文本、匹配梯度的虚构主题文本、随机字符；一种无注入对照组），每种条件8个随机种子。在训练第200步（峰值学习率阶段）将256行批次中的一行替换为194词元的固定文本注入。测量注入后50步和最终步骤的效果差异，以及插值损失障碍、留出交叉熵、各层中心核对齐、权重位移等指标。

Result: 注入后50步，接触过文本的模型分支比未接触的分支在预测该文本上优势0.039-0.044 nats（8/8种子，p<10⁻⁴）；训练结束时该差异消失（p=0.25和p=0.71）。预注册的插值损失障碍对比为+0.0068（p=0.509），留出交叉熵为-0.00044（p=0.310）。权重位移达到种子间欧氏距离的44.1%，92%在训练中点前完成；而损失障碍仅达种子间障碍的3.0%。两者相差约15倍。

Conclusion: 单个样本从一次接触中被学习后随即衰减，最终未能检测到持续影响。注入操作仅使模型在其参数盆地内重新定位，未改变盆地本身。权重位移与损失障碍之间存在约15倍的差距，表明几何测量需要在大尺度上理解模型训练动态。

Abstract: A single training example's contribution to a finished model is normally estimated rather than measured, because measuring it takes two expensive full pre-training runs that differ in one row of one batch. We ran that counterfactual 24 times at a small scale. We trained 32 GPT-2 models at 124M parameters from scratch on OpenWebText, over four conditions and eight seeds. At step 200 of 9,536, at peak learning rate, we replaced one row of a 256-row batch with a fixed context injection carrying a 194-token passage. The three injected conditions are: 1. fluent prose with a corpus-attested subject, 2. fluent prose with a fabricated subject matched to it within 0.14% on full-batch gradient delta, and 3. random keyboard characters. The fourth condition is an uninjected twin. The passage is learned from one exposure and then decays. Fifty steps after injection, the arm that saw a passage predicts it better than the arm that did not by 0.039 and 0.044 nats of cross-entropy on the passage, at eight of eight seeds with p < $10^{-4}$. At the final step we do not detect that difference for either passage, at p = 0.25 and p = 0.71, against minimum detectable effects of 0.025 and 0.079 nats, nor between the two passages, at p=0.54. Every geometric measure we report is taken after that decay. Our pre-registered contrast on interpolation loss barrier is +0.0068 with p = 0.509, against a minimum detectable effect of 0.032 barrier units. Held-out cross-entropy is $-0.00044$ with p = 0.310. Per-layer centered kernel alignment does not detectably separate any condition at any layer. Weight displacement reaches 44.1% of the seed-to-seed Euclidean distance and is 92% settled by the midpoint of training, while the barrier reaches 3.0% of the seed-to-seed barrier. Those two figures sit roughly 15 times apart, and that is a lower bound. The injection relocates the model within its basin without moving it out.

</details>


### [90] [Lévy Attention: Single-Pass Predictive Uncertainty for Continuous-Time Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19171)
*Sotirios P. Chatzis,Loukas Papadoulas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了Lévy Attention，一种能够为不规则采样时间序列的连续时间点预测提供免费、封闭形式不确定性估计的交叉注意力算子，在不损失准确性的前提下解决了深度模型缺乏可信度评估的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的不规则采样时间序列深度模型能够在任意连续时间戳进行预测，但无法报告每个预测的可信程度或不确定性，限制了模型在关键应用中的可靠性。

Method: 引入Lévy Attention，将其输出建模为关于非齐次泊松随机测度的随机积分。查询-键兼容性在连续(时间×通道)索引空间上组装强度，泊松测度在其下散布原子，输出为插值值场在这些原子处的平均。数学上，利用证据Λq和分歧tr ΣV(q)的组合构造均方根偏差σ̂(q)作为不确定性的封闭形式估计，期望层面退化为磨光余弦核注意力，可直接替代softmax层进行训练。

Result: 在t-PatchGNN模型上，该算子相比匹配对照组最多仅损失5.6%准确率，在最稀疏数据集上无损失。分歧信号携带主要信息，证据因子在稀疏数据上更具信息性。不确定性估计在匹配设置下优于20次MC Dropout，零样本CRPS优于50次采样器，分割共形包装器在各置信水平达到标称覆盖率，单次前向传播可在1.4秒内对3,383名患者进行信任排序。

Conclusion: Lévy Attention成功地将不确定性估计嵌入到注意力机制本身，实现了无需额外训练头或多次前向传播的免费、封闭形式可信度报告，为深度时间序列模型提供了理论可靠且计算高效的不确定性量化方案。

Abstract: Deep models for irregularly-sampled time series answer queries at arbitrary continuous timestamps, yet report nothing about how far each answer should be trusted. We show the attention layer itself can close that gap: with the right stochastic formulation, the pass that makes each prediction also reports, in closed form and at no extra cost, how far it should be trusted. We introduce Lévy Attention, a cross-attention operator whose output is a stochastic integral against an inhomogeneous Poisson random measure: query-key compatibilities assemble an intensity over a continuous (time x channel) index space, the measure scatters atoms under it, and the output averages an interpolated value field at those atoms. In expectation it reduces to a mollified cosine-kernel attention, so it replaces a softmax layer and trains with exact gradients.
  What softmax discards, the Poisson construction preserves in closed form: the evidence $Λ_q$ (total compatibility mass) and the disagreement $\mathrm{tr}\,Σ_V(q)$ (value spread). An exact variance identity makes their combination $\hatσ(q)=\sqrt{\mathrm{tr}\,Σ_V(q)\,\varphi(Λ_q)}$ the root-mean-square deviation of the sampled operator, emitted by the deterministic pass with no trained head.
  Empirically, disagreement carries the signal, while the evidence factor swings from uninformative on dense data to strongly informative on sparse. On t-PatchGNN the operator swap costs at most 5.6% accuracy against a matched control and nothing on the sparsest dataset. The free disagreement signal improves on 20-pass MC dropout across matched five-seed suites, and $\hatσ$ scales a calibrated Gaussian whose zero-sample CRPS beats a fifty-draw sampler; a split-conformal wrapper reaches nominal coverage at every level, and one pass ranks 3,383 unseen patients by trust in 1.4 seconds.

</details>


### [91] [Beyond Teacher Likelihood: Group-Calibrated On-Policy Distillation for Long-Context Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19181)
*Zhu Zhang,Jixun Wang,Xiaoang Xu,Xiaorong Wang,Zihan Zhou,Zhiyuan Wang,Shuo Wang,Chaojun Xiao,Yuezhi Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出GC-OPD方法，通过组级标准化和相对优势分配解决长上下文任务中在线策略蒸馏教师与任务验证器的不一致问题，显著提升Qwen3模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在长上下文任务中，传统的在线策略蒸馏（OPD）使用密集的token级教师指导，但这种方法可能偏好局部合理的响应，而忽略分布在输入中的证据或违反全局任务约束。与之相对，任务特定验证器在响应级别评估任务完成度。作者发现在更长的输入范围内，轨迹级OPD分数与验证器奖励逐渐不一致，存在教师-验证器分歧问题。

Method: 提出组校准在线策略蒸馏（GC-OPD）方法：1）在每个展开组内分别标准化验证器奖励和轨迹级OPD分数；2）使用两者差值作为有符号的教师-验证器分歧残差；3）通过相对优势信用分配（RACA）根据token的相对OPD优势将轨迹级残差分配到各token，同时保留原始OPD信号。

Result: 在五个长上下文基准测试中，GC-OPD将Qwen3-4B和Qwen3-8B的五基准平均值分别从29.08提升至40.47和从35.12提升至44.65。相同设置下，普通OPD仅达到39.31和43.56。消融实验验证了有符号残差比额外的OPD项或直接的组标准化验证器奖励更有效，RACA比均匀token分配更优。

Conclusion: 组相对残差校准可以有效整合验证器结果，同时保留密集的token级指导，解决了长上下文任务中教师与验证器不一致的问题，为知识蒸馏提供了新的校准框架。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) trains a student on its own responses using dense token-level guidance from a stronger teacher. In long-context tasks, however, token-level teacher support can favor locally plausible responses that omit evidence distributed across the input or violate global task constraints. Task-specific verifiers, in contrast, evaluate task completion at the response level and may return graded rewards that reflect partial success. We diagnose this mismatch on fixed responses from two representative long-context evidence-aggregation tasks. Across longer input ranges, trajectory-level OPD scores become progressively less aligned with verifier rewards, indicating teacher-verifier disagreement. Motivated by this observation, we introduce Group-Calibrated On-Policy Distillation (GC-OPD). GC-OPD separately normalizes verifier rewards and trajectory-level OPD scores within each rollout group and uses their difference as a signed teacher-verifier disagreement residual. Relative-advantage-based credit assignment (RACA) distributes this trajectory-level residual across tokens according to their relative OPD advantages while preserving the original OPD signal. Across five long-context benchmarks, post-training with GC-OPD raises the five-benchmark averages of the official Qwen3-4B and Qwen3-8B checkpoints from 29.08 to 40.47 and from 35.12 to 44.65, respectively. Vanilla OPD reaches 39.31 and 43.56 under the same setup. Controlled ablations show that the signed residual is more effective than either an additional OPD-derived term or direct group-normalized verifier reward addition, while RACA further improves over uniform token allocation. Together, these results demonstrate that group-relative residual calibration can incorporate verifier outcomes without discarding dense token-level guidance. Code is available at https://github.com/SolereZhang/GC-OPD.

</details>


<div id='cs.MA'></div>

# cs.MA [[Back]](#toc)

### [92] [Contracting for LLM Delegation: Moral Hazard in Technology and Effort Choice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18232)
*Nanda Kishore Sreenivas,Kate Larson*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文扩展了委托-代理框架，应用于代理选择技术（如大语言模型）和努力水平的场景，推导出最优线性合同并通过LLM基准测试验证了理论均衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在大语言模型时代，智能代理需要在多种模型和努力水平（如token预算）之间做出选择，传统的委托-代理框架无法有效处理这种两维隐藏行动问题，因此需要扩展框架以研究技术选择与努力分配的激励设计。

Method: 将产出质量与努力的关系建模为依赖于代理两维隐藏行动（技术选择和努力分配）的凹型饱和函数，推导委托人的最优线性合同，分析代理人的最佳响应和技术切换阈值，并使用bandit算法在MATH和MMLUPro基准上校准验证模型。

Result: 推导出了最优线性合同，证明了代理的最佳响应由触发技术切换的阈值奖励份额决定；实验显示委托方和代理方使用bandit算法时，其策略收敛于理论均衡，简单的线性合同能够有效激励复杂的、技术感知的委托流程。

Conclusion: 简单的线性合同可以有效激励代理在工作流中进行复杂的技术感知委托决策，理论推导与实验验证高度吻合，为智能体系统中的激励机制设计提供了理论指导。

Abstract: We extend the standard Principal-Agent framework to scenarios where the Agent selects from a suite of technologies, each characterized by a distinct cost-capability profile. This framework is increasingly critical in the era of Large Language Models (LLMs), where Agents choose both a model and an associated effort level (e.g., token budget). We model the relationship between output quality and effort as a concave, saturating function, which depends on the Agent's hidden two-dimensional action choice balancing technology selection and effort allocation. We derive the optimal linear contract for the Principal, demonstrating that the Agent's best response is characterized by a threshold reward share that triggers technology switching. Finally, we calibrate our model using open-weight LLM pairings across the MATH and MMLUPro benchmarks. We show that both Principal and Agent, when employing bandit algorithms to navigate this environment, converge to strategies that closely align with our theoretical equilibrium. These results suggest that simple linear contracts can effectively incentivize complex, technology-aware delegation in agentic workflows.

</details>


### [93] [A Locally Deployable Tool-Grounded LLM Multi-agent Framework for Automating Methane Emission Analysis and Reporting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18473)
*Yang Yan,Zifan Zhou,Xuan Wang,Erum Hassan,Bilguunzaya Mijiddorj,Jie Cao,Bin Li,Binbin Weng*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本研究开发了一个基于工具接地大语言模型的多智能体框架，用于低成本甲烷传感和现场监测活动，将工作流程时间从小时级缩短至分钟级，并在实际部署中实现了92.0%的工作流路由准确率和85.0%的排放率估计成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 甲烷现场监测需要整合采样设计、气象解释、传感器处理、羽流分析、可视化和报告等多个步骤，但这些步骤通常分散在单独的专家驱动工作流程中，导致效率低下和协调困难。现有的手动和通用LLM辅助工作流程存在耗时长、人工协调需求高、数据安全风险等问题。

Method: 开发了一个本地可部署的工具接地大语言模型多智能体框架，使用LLM代理作为工作流程协调器，连接现场测量、气象数据、确定性传感器处理例程、高斯羽流反演和报告生成。该框架采用工具接地方式，LLM代理负责工作流协调而非直接估计甲烷浓度或排放。

Result: 在废水处理设施、垃圾填埋场和油气站点等多种真实环境中进行了广泛现场部署，实现了：工作流路由和参数提取准确率92.0%；排放率估计和羽流预测成功率85.0%；可编辑报告生成成功率95.0%。与手动和通用LLM辅助工作流程相比，将工作流程时间从小时级减少到分钟级，降低了人工协调和提示工程要求，并保留了可追溯的基于羽流的输出。大多数处理可在本地执行，减少了敏感设施数据暴露给云服务的风险。

Conclusion: 工具接地的LLM协调能够有效降低甲烷现场监测的时间、劳动力、可用性和数据安全障碍，为环境监测领域提供了一个高效、可本地部署的解决方案。该框架证明了LLM在复杂多步骤工作流程协调中的实用价值，同时保障了数据隐私和处理效率。

Abstract: Methane field monitoring requires the integration of sampling design, meteorological interpretation, sensor processing, plume analysis, visualization, and reporting, but these steps are often distributed across separate expert-driven workflows. We developed a locally deployable, tool-grounded large language model (LLM) multi-agent framework for our low-cost methane sensing and field-monitoring campaigns. The framework uses LLM agents as workflow coordinators that link field measurements, meteorological data, deterministic sensor-processing routines, Gaussian plume inversion, and report generation, rather than directly estimating methane concentrations or emissions. Extensive field deployments across diverse real-world environments (e.g., wastewater treatment facilities, landfills, and oil and gas sites) demonstrate that our framework can achieve 92.0\% accuracy in workflow routing and parameter extraction, 85.0\% success in emission-rate estimation and plume prediction, and 95.0\% success in generating editable reports under practical operating conditions. Compared with manual and general-purpose LLM-assisted workflows, it reduced workflow time from hours-level to minutes-level, lowered manual coordination and prompt-engineering requirements, and retained traceable plume-based outputs. In addition, most processing can be performed locally, reducing exposure of sensitive facility and field data to cloud services. These results indicate that tool-grounded LLM coordination can reduce the time, labor, usability, and data-security barriers of methane field monitoring.

</details>


### [94] [Bayesian Partner Modelling enables Adaptive Replanning for LLM Coordination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18490)
*Harsh Goel,Aditya Sai Ellendula,Vaishnav Tadiparthi,Ehsan Moradi Pari,Hossein Nourkhiz Mahjoub,Sandeep P. Chinchali*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 提出BayesBeliefAgent，通过贝叶斯跟踪模块与分层LLM规划器结合，仅在检测到合作伙伴技能矛盾时中断当前技能，大幅减少重规划次数并显著缩小信念-行动差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体大语言模型系统在与策略发生中期转变的新队友协作时表现不佳。由于智能体执行多步骤或时序扩展技能，往往在公开证据表明合作伙伴已改变技能后仍继续执行过时计划。现有方法要么被动地将合作伙伴跟踪视为上下文（导致智能体意识到变化但行动迟缓），要么无差别地进行重规划。

Method: 引入BayesBeliefAgent，将分层LLM规划器与贝叶斯跟踪模块配对。该智能体不再持续重规划，而是仅在合作伙伴的行动直接与推断的技能相矛盾时才中断当前技能，实现矛盾条件控制。

Result: 在Overcooked基准环境中，矛盾条件控制大幅缩小信念-行动差距（定义为智能体拥有正确伙伴估计但执行非互补技能的决策比例），同时比启发式方法减少一个数量级的重规划次数。

Conclusion: BayesBeliefAgent通过矛盾条件控制机制，在多智能体协作中实现了高效的自适应能力，在保持协作性能的同时显著提升了重规划效率，为处理合作伙伴策略动态变化提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Multi-agent Large Language Model (LLM) systems often struggle to collaborate with new teammates whose strategies shift mid-task. Because agents execute multi-step or temporally extended skills, they frequently continue executing outdated plans long after public evidence shows that a partner has changed its skill. Existing methods either treat partner tracking as passive context-leaving the agent aware of the shift but slow to act-or replan indiscriminately. We introduce BayesBeliefAgent, which pairs a hierarchical LLM planner with a Bayesian tracking module. Rather than replanning constantly, our agent interrupts its current skill only when a partner's actions directly contradict the inferred skill. Beyond standard reward, we evaluate performance using replanning efficiency and the belief-action gap: the fraction of total decisions where an agent with a correct partner estimate executes a non-complementary skill. Across benchmark Overcooked environments, contradiction-conditioned control drastically narrows this belief-action gap while requiring an order of magnitude fewer replans than heuristic methods

</details>


<div id='cs.DC'></div>

# cs.DC [[Back]](#toc)

### [95] [When Do Microservices Save Energy? Evidence from Environmental Simulation Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18376)
*Joshua Rowley,Abdessalam Elhabbash*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 微服务架构对环境模拟模型能耗影响的研究，发现对于小型或紧耦合模型会增加能耗，但事件驱动编排和选择性重执行可显著降低大型工作流的能耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 环境模拟模型需要反复执行以支持场景分析、校准和决策制定，但会带来显著的能源成本。虽然微服务提供了模块化和可扩展性，但由于分解会引入编排、通信、持久化和空闲服务开销，其对低碳排放的影响尚不明确。

Method: 将四个环境模型作为容器化的微服务工作流进行评估，对比单体执行、轮询式编排和事件驱动编排三种模式的能耗表现。

Result: 微服务会增加小型或紧耦合模型的能耗（协调开销占主导）；对于大型工作流，事件驱动编排尽管运行时间更长却能减少能耗；选择性下游重执行在重复参数探索中实现了41%的能耗降低。

Conclusion: 微服务架构的能效取决于模型规模和耦合度，大型工作流采用事件驱动编排和优化重执行策略可实现显著的节能减排效果。

Abstract: Environmental simulation models support scenario analysis, calibration, and decision-making, but repeated execution can incur significant energy costs. Microservices offer modularity and scalability, yet their low-carbon impact remains unclear because decomposition introduces orchestration, communication, persistence, and idle-service overheads. This paper evaluates four environmental models as containerised microservice workflows, comparing monolithic execution with polling-based and event-driven orchestration. Results show that microservices increase energy consumption for smaller or tightly coupled models, where coordination overhead dominates. For a larger workflow, event-driven orchestration reduces energy use despite longer runtime, while selective downstream re-execution achieves a 41% reduction during repeated parameter exploration.

</details>


### [96] [SLO-Scaler: Uncertainty-Aware SLO-Driven Autoscaling for Microservices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18390)
*Shuo Wang,Xiaoxuan Sun,Shao-yu Huang,Bencheng Su,Shuo Xu,Netra Awate*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出SLO-Scaler框架，通过贝叶斯LSTM预测SLO违规概率并分析服务依赖，在突发流量下将SLO违规率降低29-56%，同时减少18-33%的副本数量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Kubernetes HPA等阈值方法对突发流量响应迟缓，预测性方法忽视预测不确定性导致过度配置或扩缩容震荡。微服务应用因突发负载、级联延迟和冷启动开销难以满足SLO。

Method: 提出SLO-Scaler框架：1) 使用贝叶斯LSTM模型预测短期请求率、尾延迟和SLO违规概率，输出置信区间；2) 基于置信区间的扩缩容决策；3) 依赖图分析模块定位瓶颈服务，避免不必要的服务链整体扩容。

Result: 在DeathStarBench社交网络基准测试上，突发流量场景下：SLO违规率降低29-56%，平均副本数减少18-33%，扩缩容事件频率降低38-59%，尾延迟更低。

Conclusion: SLO-Scaler通过不确定性感知预测和精准的瓶颈服务定位，实现了更高效的资源利用和更可靠的SLO保障，显著优于现有基线方法。

Abstract: Autoscaling microservice-based applications to satisfy Service Level Objectives (SLOs) remains challenging due to bursty workloads, cascading latency across service dependencies, and cold-start overhead. Existing approaches such as the Kubernetes Horizontal Pod Autoscaler (HPA) rely on threshold-based CPU or memory metrics, which react too slowly to traffic spikes. Recent predictive methods improve responsiveness but generate point forecasts that ignore prediction uncertainty, leading to over-provisioning or oscillatory scaling. We propose SLO-Scaler, an uncertainty-aware autoscaling framework that predicts short-horizon request rates, tail latency, and SLO violation probability using a Bayesian LSTM model. SLO-Scaler integrates confidence-interval-based scaling decisions with a dependency graph analysis module that localizes bottleneck services, avoiding unnecessary whole-chain scaling. We evaluate SLO-Scaler on the DeathStarBench Social Network benchmark deployed on Kubernetes under periodic, bursty, and long-tail traffic patterns. Under bursty traffic, SLO-Scaler reduces the SLO violation rate by 29-56%, lowers the average replica count by 18-33%, and decreases scaling event frequency by 38-59% compared with the baselines, while achieving lower tail latency.

</details>


### [97] [A Simple Construction of Locally Checkable Problems Filling the LOCAL Complexity Gaps in Graphs with Arbitrary Large Degrees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18684)
*Filippo Casagrande,Pierre Fraigniaud,Benjamin Jauregui,Mikaël Rabie*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文证明了LCL问题的复杂度间隙源于最大度数的有界性，而非局部可检查性本身，通过设计Increasing Degree问题在无界度网络中实现了$[ω(1),o(\log\log^\star n)]$和$[ω(\log^\star n),o(\log n)]$区间的复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前研究表明LCL问题存在复杂度间隙，但这些结果受限于多项式复杂度区域或树结构。本文旨在证明这些间隙的产生根源在于LCL问题的定义要求最大度数有界，而非局部可检查性本身，从而扩展到更一般的局部可检查问题。

Method: 引入参数化的Increasing Degree问题，通过调整参数函数$f$获得不同复杂度；提出Translation Theorem用于将结果从一个复杂度区间转移到更低复杂度区间；上界使用端口编号模型下的确定性算法，下界针对随机化LOCAL甚至量子LOCAL模型。

Result: 在无界最大度网络中，存在无穷多个局部可检查问题的复杂度属于$[ω(1),o(\log\log^\star n)]$和$[ω(\log^\star n),o(\log n)]$区间，扩展了之前仅适用于多项式复杂度和树结构的结论。

Conclusion: LCL问题的复杂度间隙完全归因于最大度数的有界约束，而非局部可检查性；该结论通过Increasing Degree问题和Translation Theorem在弱模型（上界）到强模型（下界）间建立了完整的复杂度层次。

Abstract: We show that the complexity gaps in the round complexities of locally checkable labeling (LCL) problems are not due to the fact that solutions to LCL problems must be locally checkable, but solely to the fact that LCL problems are defined only for graphs of maximum degree upper bounded by some arbitrary yet constant value $Δ$. Specifically, we show that there are infinitely many locally checkable problems (i.e., problems whose solutions can be checked locally) whose round complexities belongs to the two intervals $[ω(1),o(\log\log^\star n)]$ and $[ω(\log^\star n),o(\log n)]$ whenever these problems are considered in networks with unbounded maximum degrees. This extends the previous results by Schmid (arXiv, 2026), which hold for the polynomial regime only, and by Bousquet, Feuilloley, and Pierron (OPODIS, 2025), which hold for trees only.
  All our upper bounds are obtained using deterministic algorithms that can be run under the port-numbering model, which is a weak variant of LOCAL, without any a priori information on the number of nodes in the network. Instead, our lower bounds apply to randomized LOCAL, and quantum LOCAL, even if nodes have identifiers in $[1,n]$, and even if they know the exact number of nodes in the network. They even hold under randomized online LOCAL, a strong variant of the LOCAL model. Finally, our lower bounds hold even for trees.
  Our results are obtained using two main ingredients. The first one is the analysis of a new locally checkable problem called Increasing Degree, parameterized by a function $f:\mathbb{N}\to\mathbb{N}$. Different round complexities can be obtained by tuning the function $f$ accordingly. Our second tool is a general Translation Theorem that enables to transfer results from a given range of complexities to results for a range of lower complexities.

</details>


### [98] [Enabling Reuse for Data-Sharing Pipelines in Federated Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18751)
*Sepideh Masoudi,Maria C. Borges,Eduardo Brito,Sebastian Werner,Stefan Tai*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向复用的数据共享流水线设计方法，通过系统性地重用现有数据资产和转换逻辑，应对联邦数据网格中流水线激增的挑战，从而减少冗余并提高可管理性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据网格架构通过领域拥有的数据产品实现去中心化数据共享，但在联邦环境中支持多样化消费者往往需要定制化的数据共享流水线。随着消费者数量增长，流水线大量增加，导致设计和维护复杂度急剧上升。作者观察到这些流水线经常存在显著的结构重叠，因此提出将复用作为指导原则来应对这一挑战。

Method: 将数据共享流水线中的复用定义为现有数据资产和转换逻辑在流水线间的系统性使用，识别了设计时和运行时两个层面的复用机会。分析了相关挑战，提出了一种面向复用的设计方法，并提供了相应的参考架构。通过初步评估验证了该方法的可行性。

Result: 初步评估表明，复用方法具有减少冗余和提高可管理性的潜力，为更具可扩展性和可持续性的联邦数据共享提供了可行的解决路径。

Conclusion: 复用原则能够有效缓解联邦数据共享环境中流水线激增带来的复杂度问题，为实现更可持续的数据网格架构提供了重要的设计指导和技术支撑。

Abstract: Data mesh architectures enable decentralized data sharing through domain-owned data products, but supporting diverse consumers in federated settings often requires customized data-sharing pipelines. As the number of consumers grows, this leads to a proliferation of pipelines, increasing design and maintenance complexity. We observe that such pipelines frequently exhibit substantial structural overlap. In this paper, we argue that reuse should serve as a guiding principle to address this challenge. We define reuse in data-sharing pipelines as the systematic use of existing data assets and transformation logic across pipelines, and identify reuse opportunities at both design time and runtime. We analyze the associated challenges and outline a reuse-oriented design approach, supported by a reference architecture. A preliminary evaluation demonstrates the potential of reuse to reduce redundancy and improve manageability, providing a pathway toward more scalable and sustainable federated data sharing.

</details>


### [99] [Tight Energy Lower Bounds for Distributed Graph Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18992)
*Fabien Dufoulon,Gopal Pandurangan,Peter Robinson*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于信息论的能量复杂度下界证明技术，并证明了多个基础图问题的能量复杂度下界与其时间复杂度下界相匹配，无法获得显著能量优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前研究表明，领导选举、广播、最小生成树、极大独立集等问题在SLEEPING模型中的能量复杂度相比CONGEST模型中的时间复杂度有指数级降低。这引发了一个根本问题：是否许多其他基础问题也能获得如此显著的能量增益？本文旨在回答这一问题。

Method: 提出了一种通用的基于信息论的能量下界证明技术。该技术提供了一种"即插即用"的方式来展示CONGEST模型中各种问题的能量下界。通过利用已知的通信复杂度下界，获得新的几乎最优（仅相差对数因子）的能量复杂度下界。

Result: 对于三角形枚举、所有点对最短路径（APSP）、直径计算、最小权重环、最大独立集（MaxIS）、最小支配集（MinDS）、最小顶点覆盖（MinVC）等基础图问题，证明了多项式级别（关于n）的能量复杂度下界，包括最坏情况和平均情况。这些问题的能量下界与其各自的时间下界相匹配。

Conclusion: 对于上述基础图问题，无法在能量复杂度上获得任何显著增益。这表明并非所有问题都能像领导选举、MST等问题那样在SLEEPING模型中获得指数级的能量优化，揭示了能量优化潜力的局限性。

Abstract: There has been a significant recent interest in designing distributed algorithms in the SLEEPING model that minimize the {energy (a.k.a awake) complexity, which measures the number of rounds a node is awake during the algorithm. A node spends non-trivial resources (messages, energy, etc.) only when it is awake and not while sleeping. Energy complexity has been studied for various fundamental problems with respect to minimizing the maximum (worst-case) or the average number of rounds a node is awake.
  It has been shown that the energy complexities of several fundamental problems such as leader election (LE), broadcast, Minimum Spanning Tree (MST), Maximal Independent Set (MIS) is exponentially smaller compared to their respective best-possible round complexities in the standard CONGEST model (where nodes can only send messages of small size). This raises a fundamental question of whether such significant energy gains are possible for many other fundamental problems.
  Our main contribution is a general and powerful technique for showing energy lower bounds using information theory. It gives almost a "plug-in" way to show energy lower bounds for various problems in the standard CONGEST model. Our information-theoretic technique allows us to leverage known lower bounds on communication complexity to obtain new, almost optimal (up to logarithmic factors) polynomial (in $n$) lower bounds on energy complexity --- for both worst-case and average-case --- for fundamental graph problems such as triangle enumeration, All-Pairs Shortest Paths (APSP), diameter computation, minimum weight cycle, Maximum Independent Set (MaxIS), Minimum Dominating Set (MinDS), Minimum Vertex Cover (MinVC). The energy lower bounds of these problems match their respective round lower bounds, implying that one cannot obtain any significant gains in energy complexity.

</details>


### [100] [Approximating Minimum Dominating Set with Few Awake Rounds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19096)
*Hongyan Ji,Shreyas Pai,Sriram V. Pemmaraju*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文在睡眠CONGEST模型下首次实现了MDS问题的O(log Δ)近似算法，将唤醒复杂度从Ω(log²Δ)降低到Õ(log Δ)，同时保持O(log²Δ)的轮数复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MDS问题的O(log Δ)近似算法虽然能在O(log²Δ)轮内完成，但唤醒复杂度均为Ω(log²Δ)。在睡眠CONGEST模型中，节点只能在唤醒状态下进行计算和通信，因此降低唤醒复杂度对于节能和实际应用具有重要意义。本文的核心问题是如何在保持近似比的同时降低唤醒复杂度。

Method: 本文首先将Grunau等人的CONGEST模型SetCover算法推广到双参数版本，该算法可在O(log_pΔ·log_qΔ)轮内计算O(p·q·log_pΔ)近似支配集。在此基础上，通过采样估计和虚拟二叉树调度等技术，设计了适用于睡眠CONGEST模型的算法。参数化方法允许在近似比和唤醒复杂度之间进行权衡。

Result: 主要结果包括：(1)首次提出O(log Δ)近似算法，运行O(log²Δ)轮，唤醒复杂度为Õ(log Δ)；(2)对于任意1<α≤Δ，提出O(αlog Δ)近似算法，运行Õ(logΔ·log_αΔ)轮，唤醒复杂度为Õ(log_αΔ)；(3)开发了双参数SetCover算法，可在O(log_pΔ·log_qΔ)轮内计算O(p·q·log_pΔ)近似支配集。

Conclusion: 本文突破了MDS问题在睡眠CONGEST模型中唤醒复杂度的下界障碍，首次实现了次平方级的唤醒复杂度。通过参数化方法揭示了近似比与唤醒复杂度之间的权衡关系。所提出的双参数SetCover算法和虚拟二叉树调度技术为睡眠模型下的分布式算法设计提供了新工具。

Abstract: We study the Minimum Dominating Set (MDS) problem in the sleeping CONGEST model (Chatterjee, Gmyr, and Pandurangan, PODC 2020), a generalization of the standard CONGEST model, in which a node may sleep in some rounds and can only compute, send messages, or receive messages when it is awake. The awake complexity of an algorithm in this model is the worst case number (over all inputs and all nodes) of rounds a node is awake for during the execution of the algorithm. While there are several $O(\log Δ)$-approximation algorithms (in expectation) for MDS that run in $O(\log^2 Δ)$ rounds, all of these have $Ω(\log^2 Δ)$ awake complexity. Whether this awake complexity can be improved is the question that drives our work. We present the first $O(\log Δ)$-approximation algorithm for MDS with $o(\log^2 Δ)$ awake complexity; our algorithm runs in $O(\log^2Δ)$ rounds with $\tilde{O}(\logΔ)$ awake complexity. We can reduce the awake complexity further, but at the cost of approximation: we present, for any $1<α\leΔ$, an algorithm in the sleeping CONGEST model that computes an $O(α\logΔ)$-approximate dominating set in expectation in $\tilde{O}(\logΔ\cdot \log_α Δ)$ rounds with $\tilde{O}(\log_α Δ)$ awake complexity. Our results depend on a generalization of the CONGEST model SetCover algorithm of Grunau, Mitrovi'c, Rubinfeld, and Vakilian (SODA 2020) that we develop. This generalization computes an $O(p\cdot q\cdot\log_pΔ)$-approximate dominating set in $O(\log_pΔ\cdot\log_qΔ)$ rounds for parameters $1<p,q\leΔ$. Our sleeping CONGEST algorithms apply a variety of techniques including sampling-based estimation and scheduling using virtual binary trees to the aforementioned 2-parameter SetCover algorithm.

</details>


### [101] [Pre-Compiled Pipeline Shards for Distributed LLM Inference on Intel AI PC Fleets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19147)
*Tate Berenbaum,Muthaiah Venkatachalam*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种利用多台普通AI PC协同推理大语言模型的方案，通过流水线并行将模型分片，结合OpenVINO优化、推测解码和微批次技术，实现了在消费级硬件上运行超越单机能力的LLM（如70B参数模型）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代Intel AI PC配备了16GB以上统一内存的集成GPU和NPU，但大量时间处于空闲状态。然而，这些内存不足以容纳大模型（如70B参数的LLM）。为了充分利用这些闲置的分布式计算资源，需要一种方法让多台AI PC协同工作，突破单机内存限制。

Method: 采用三种关键技术：(1) **流水线并行**：将模型按层分片为多个阶段，每个阶段预编译为OpenVINO图，每台机器运行一个分片并将激活传递给下一台；(2) **优化技术**：通过在每个分片中注入beam_idx Gather触发IndirectKVCache融合，恢复未分割模型的推理速度；(3) **推测解码**：在状态化OpenVINO模型上实现推测解码；(4) **微批次**：通过交错处理多个用户请求实现并发服务，每个请求携带自己的缓存。

Result: 实验结果表明：双节点Llama 3.1 8B INT4流水线以1.79倍于同硬件单用户未分割模型的吞吐量服务两个并发用户；在模拟广域网延迟下差距进一步扩大。该设计成功扩展到70B模型——四节点Lunar Lake AI PC部署在Intel Tiber Cloud上，能以交互速度服务单用户，输出与无推测解码的四节点流水线逐token一致。

Conclusion: 证明了通过网络协作的普通AI PC集群可以服务超越任何单个成员能力的大模型，为充分利用闲置消费级硬件资源提供了可行方案，代码、基准日志和复现脚本已开源发布。

Abstract: Modern Intel AI PCs ship capable integrated GPUs and NPUs with 16+ GB of unified memory, and they spend considerable time idle. That is not enough memory to fit a large model such as a 70B-parameter LLM. We show that a handful of AIPCs, working together over an ordinary network, can serve models beyond the capability of any single one. We use pipeline parallelism: a model is split by layer into per-stage shards, each pre-compiled into an OpenVINO graph, so that every machine runs one shard and passes activations to the next. Three techniques make this fast enough to be useful. First, we recover the speed of the unsplit model: a naive per-stage export runs well below monolithic inference because it misses an OpenVINO GPU optimization, and injecting a beam_idx Gather into each shard triggers that optimization (the IndirectKVCache fusion) and brings the shards to parity. Second, we leverage speculative decoding on stateful OpenVINO models. Third, the pipeline serves several users at once by interleaving their requests across the stages, each request carrying its own cache (micro-batching). Together, a two-node Llama 3.1 8B INT4 pipeline serves two concurrent users at 1.79x the single-user throughput of the unsplit model on the same hardware, and the gap widens under simulated wide-area latency. The same design scales to a 70B model that no single fleet member can hold: a four-node deployment of Lunar Lake AI PCs on Intel Tiber Cloud serves a single user at interactive speed, with output token-for-token identical to the same four-node pipeline decoding without speculation. Code, raw benchmark logs, and reproduction scripts ship as a self-contained package at https://github.com/labscommunity/pipeline-sharded-inference-paper (in the top-level reproduction/ directory).

</details>


### [102] [Upper and Lower Bounds on the Space Complexity of Multi-word Single-Writer Registers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19167)
*Yuanhao Wei,Yousof Yavari*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文证明了使用小寄存器模拟大共享寄存器的空间复杂度的匹配上下界，其中下界对无阻塞实现成立，上界通过无等待算法实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在分布式共享内存系统中，理解如何使用小的寄存器构建大的寄存器是基础性问题。之前已知的上下界之间存在差距，特别是下界不够强。本文旨在为单写入者场景建立更紧确的空间复杂度界限。

Method: (1) 证明下界：对于不可见读者的无阻塞实现，证明下界为⌈(m-1)/(b-1)⌉；对于一般情况，证明下界为⌈min((m-1)/(b-1), r+log m/log b)⌉，其中假设基寄存器是原子的而被模拟寄存器是规则的。(2) 构造上界：开发无等待算法，使用Θ(r + log m/log b)空间实现多字原子寄存器；结合已知的不可见读者构造方法得到Θ(min(m/b, r + log m/log b))空间上界。

Result: 下界结果：(1) 不可见读者情况：⌈(m-1)/(b-1)⌉个基寄存器，比此前最佳下界有指数级改进；(2) 一般情况：⌈min((m-1)/(b-1), r+log m/log b)⌉个基寄存器。上界结果：Θ(min(m/b, r + log m/log b))空间，改进了此前Θ(min(m/b, r·log m/log b))的上界。上下界在渐进意义上匹配。

Conclusion: 本文完整刻画了使用小寄存器模拟大寄存器的空间复杂度。下界在较强的条件下成立（基寄存器原子、被模拟寄存器规则、无阻塞实现），而构造的上界算法是无等待的，这表明所建立的界限具有很强的普适性。这些结果填补了该领域长期存在的理论空白。

Abstract: We prove matching upper and lower bounds on the space complexity of simulating a large shared register using smaller shared registers. We focus on the case where both the simulated and base registers are single-writer, which means they can be accessed concurrently by multiple readers but only by a single writer. To strengthen our lower bounds, we prove that they hold even when the base registers are atomic and the simulated register is regular. Furthermore, the lower bounds hold for obstruction-free implementations, which means they also hold for lock-free and wait-free implementations.
  If $m$ is the number of values representable by the large register and $b$ is the number of values representable by each base register, our first lower bound says that any obstruction-free implementation that has an invisible reader requires at least $\lceil \frac{m-1}{b-1} \rceil$ base registers. A reader is considered invisible if it never writes to base registers. This lower bound is asymptotically tight for the invisible-reader case and represents an exponential improvement over the previous best known lower bound. For the general case, which allows any combination of visible and invisible readers, we prove a $\lceil \min(\frac{m-1}{b-1}, r+\frac{\log{m}}{\log{b}}) \rceil$ space lower bound, where $r$ is the number of readers.
  To show that this lower bound is asymptotically tight, we develop a wait-free algorithm for simulating a multi-word atomic register from atomic base registers using $Θ(r + \frac{\log{m}}{\log{b}})$ space. Combining this algorithm with known invisible-reader constructions gives a $Θ(\min(\frac{m}{b}, r + \frac{\log{m}}{\log{b}}))$ space upper bound. This improves upon the previously known space upper bound of $Θ(\min(\frac{m}{b}, r\frac{\log{m}}{\log{b}}))$.

</details>


### [103] [A Fast Deterministic Algorithm for $(Δ+1)$-edge coloring in CONGEST](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19184)
*Sebastian Brandt,Ananth Narayanan,Alexandre Nolin*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文首次在CONGEST模型中实现了(Δ+1)-边着色的多项式时间确定性分布式算法，填补了先前研究只能在允许大消息的LOCAL模型中高效运行的空白。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Vizing定理表明任何最大度为Δ的图都可以用Δ+1种颜色进行最优边着色。近期突破性研究在LOCAL模型中实现了poly(Δ,log n)轮的确定性算法，但这些算法需要传输大消息，无法应用于更严格的CONGEST模型。如何在消息大小受限的CONGEST模型中高效求解(Δ+1)-边着色问题成为开放问题。

Method: 设计了一种适用于CONGEST模型的确定性分布式算法，通过精心设计的消息传递机制和信息压缩技术，在限制消息大小的前提下实现高效的边着色过程。

Result: 成功给出了CONGEST模型中首个poly(Δ,log n)轮的(Δ+1)-边着色确定性算法，其运行时间的n依赖度为Õ(log⁵n)，达到了LOCAL模型中已发表的最佳依赖度水平。

Conclusion: 该工作解决了CONGEST模型中(Δ+1)-边着色问题的算法设计难题，证明了在消息受限条件下仍可实现与LOCAL模型相当的计算效率，为受限通信模型中的分布式图算法研究提供了重要进展。

Abstract: Vizing's theorem states that any graph of maximum degree $Δ$ can be properly edge-colored with $Δ+ 1$ colors (which is optimal in general). A recent breakthrough result by Bernshteyn showed that such a $(Δ+ 1)$-edge coloring can be found deterministically in $poly(Δ,\log n)$ rounds in the LOCAL model of distributed computing, where $n$ denotes the number of vertices of the input graph [J. Comb. Theory 2022]. Since then, the exponent in the $poly(\log n)$-part of the runtime has been improved by Christiansen [STOC 2023] and Bernshteyn and Dhawan [J. Comb. Theory, Series B, 2025]. However, the algorithms used in all of these works use large messages, leaving open the question for efficient algorithms in the more restrictive CONGEST model.
  We answer this question by presenting the first $poly(Δ,\log n)$-round algorithm for $(Δ+ 1)$-edge coloring in the CONGEST model. Our algorithm is deterministic and the $n$-dependency of its runtime, $\tilde{O}(\log^5 n)$, matches the best published dependency in the LOCAL model.

</details>


<div id='cs.AR'></div>

# cs.AR [[Back]](#toc)

### [104] [Optimizing Polynomial Multiplication and Fixed-Weight Sampling for HQC on ARM Cortex-M4](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18145)
*Jihoon Jang,Hanbeom Shin,Suhri Kim,Seokhie Hong,Donggeun Kwon*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文在ARM Cortex-M4上优化了HQC后量子密码算法的实现，通过减少多项式乘法中的数据移动开销、优化固定权重采样以及引入缓存策略，使密钥生成、封装和解封装的性能分别提升最多33.1%、34.6%和29.8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的HQC实现中，多项式乘法的Frobenius加性FFT蝶形运算包含大量冗余的VMOV数据移动指令，几乎占用一半的指令周期；同时固定权重采样的支持扩展操作开销较大。现有方法仅优化XOR计数可能导致总指令数增加，因此需要综合考虑指令开销的优化策略。

Method: 1) 提出脏感知寄存器分配策略和XOR操作重排序，减少多项式乘法中VMOV指令数最多48.1%，并结合FAFFT-CRT方法；为HQC-1发现更稀疏的FAFFT模数降低CRT重构成本。2) 使用谓词执行和4路展开重写固定权重采样的支持扩展，将内循环每字开销从22周期降至6周期并保持常数时间。3) 提出可选缓存策略复用公共变换和固定密钥下的哈希重计算。

Result: 在NUCLEO-L4R5ZI开发板上，相比先前最优实现，密钥生成、封装和解封装性能分别提升最多33.1%、34.6%和29.8%；缓存策略为封装和解封装带来额外最多32.7%和18.9%的性能提升。VMOV指令减少最多48.1%，固定权重采样内循环开销从22降至6周期。

Conclusion: 通过针对ARM Cortex-M4架构特点的多层次优化，包括寄存器分配策略改进、算法重构和缓存机制，显著提升了HQC算法在嵌入式平台上的性能表现，展示了后量子密码算法在资源受限设备上的优化潜力。

Abstract: In this paper, we present an optimized implementation of Hamming Quasi-Cyclic (HQC) on the ARM Cortex-M4. We optimize (i) the polynomial multiplication and (ii) the support expansion in fixed-weight sampling, and (iii) propose an optional caching strategy that reuses the public transforms and hash recomputed under a fixed key. For the polynomial multiplication, the fixed-constant multiplications in the Frobenius additive FFT (FAFFT) butterfly spend nearly half of their instructions on VMOV data movements between general-purpose and floating-point registers rather than arithmetic. Because minimizing the XOR count alone can increase the total instruction count, we propose a dirty-aware register-allocation policy and an XOR-operation reordering that reduce the VMOV count by up to 48.1% while leaving the XOR count unchanged. We apply these to a multiplication that combines prior FAFFT-CRT methods, and for HQC-1 we further find a 34% sparser FAFFT modulus that lowers the CRT reconstruction cost. For fixed-weight sampling, we rewrite the support expansion with predicated execution and 4-way unrolling, lowering the per-word cost of its inner loop from 22 to 6 cycles while remaining constant-time. On the NUCLEO-L4R5ZI board, our implementation reduces key generation, encapsulation, and decapsulation by up to 33.1%, 34.6%, and 29.8% over the faster of the two prior state-of-the-art implementations, and the optional caching yields a further reduction of up to 32.7% and 18.9% for encapsulation and decapsulation.

</details>


### [105] [TokenPowerSandbox: Evidence-Gated CPU-First Screening for Energy-Aware LLM Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18149)
*Chenxu Niu*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了TokenPowerSandbox工作流，通过结合可解释的CPU驻留投影器、短目标GPU探测和严格的验证机制，在保证预测可靠性的前提下，显著降低了LLM服务能耗配置分析的成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有能耗感知的LLM服务需要在真实请求模式下比较配置，但穷尽式目标GPU性能分析成本高昂，而廉价预测器在测量范围外可能产生危险的高置信度错误预测，亟需一种既经济又可靠的解决方案。

Method: 提出TokenPowerSandbox证据门控工作流，包含四个核心组件：（1）可解释的CPU驻留投影器；（2）短时目标GPU探测；（3）全工作负载验证；（4）防篡改的冻结前测量溯源机制。在NVIDIA H100 80GB上使用vLLM服务Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct，通过三次锚点重复和六个开发工作负载校准工作负载迁移，对冻结模型进行盲留出测试和独立预声明无重拟合确认。

Result: 在共51次冻结后运行中，能耗MAPE分别为6.23%和7.35%，Spearman等级相关系数为0.976和0.933。预声明的TTFT门控在并发度为4时通过（MAPE 9.27%），但在并发度低于4时触发弃权（MAPE 64.80%），证明了能耗精度不能作为延迟精度的保证。

Conclusion: TokenPowerSandbox成功实现了低成本、高可靠性的LLM服务能耗预测，能耗预测精度较高。但实验结果表明能耗准确性与延迟准确性不可等同，证据门控机制对于识别预测不确定性范围至关重要，可避免在高延迟风险区域产生误导性预测。

Abstract: Energy-aware LLM serving requires comparing configurations under realistic request shapes, yet exhaustive target-GPU profiling is costly and a cheap predictor can be dangerously confident outside its measured scope. We present TokenPowerSandbox, an evidence-gated workflow that combines an interpretable CPU-resident projector, short target-GPU probes, full-workload verification, and tamper-evident freeze-before-measurement provenance. On one NVIDIA H100 80GB serving Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct with vLLM, three anchor repeats and six development workloads calibrate workload transfer. The same frozen model is evaluated on a blind holdout and a separately predeclared no-refit confirmation totaling 51 post-freeze runs. Energy MAPE is 6.23% and 7.35%, with Spearman rank correlations of 0.976 and 0.933. However, a predeclared TTFT gate passes at concurrency four (9.27% MAPE) and triggers abstention below four (64.80%), showing why energy accuracy cannot certify latency.

</details>


### [106] [A Fault-Tolerant Spike-Time Interface for Approximate Agreement in Distributed Neuromorphic Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18151)
*Arman Ferdowsi,Maryam DehghanChenary,Kevin Tierney,Atakan Aral*
